- Locale parity green (I18N constant map resolved)

**Compatibility notes**

New admin-only route; no production view uses the partial yet.

**Rollback**

Revert the PR.

**Open input from WP-16**: editor fixtures go through `spec/support/dcf_js_fixtures.rb` (`dcf_js_fixture('editor', scenario, html)`, records from `dcf_fixture_record`); its guard already checks `data-dcf-editor-field-id`, `data-dcf-editor-parent-id`, `/custom_fields/N` and `/fields/N`.

**Open input from WP-18**: `spec/quality/locale_parity_spec.rb` resolves every value of `RedmineDependingCustomFields::ClientConfig::I18N` and `RedmineDependingCustomFields::DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` (when defined) through `unresolved_map_keys(tables, locales)`: a key of the plugin's en table must be in every locale's own table (no fallback), any other key must resolve in core per locale. WP-18's negative example stubs `ClientConfig::I18N` with a missing key; add the same example for `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N`. Keep the two maps separate (never merged).

**Open input from WP-21**: `DependencyPayload::MAX_BYTES` is 4,194,304. `digest` counts each value list as a set, a String default as a list of one and an Integer id as its String, and drops nil, blank entries and nested containers, so `digest(cf.value_dependencies, cf.default_value_dependencies) == stored_digest(cf)` for an untouched saved field. `stored_digest` deserializes `format_store` on every call; memoize it per record for `data-dcf-editor-base` and the dirty check.

**Open input from WP-22**: `field.dcf_dependencies_conflict` feeds `data-dcf-editor-conflict-mapping`: a Hash with the string keys `value_dependencies`, `default_value_dependencies`, `source` and `import` (nil when absent), parser-normalized and not pruned, a new copy per call, nil unless the last validation refused the current value as stale. After the transport applied an ok payload, the in-memory `value_dependencies` and `default_value_dependencies` hold the pruned posted mapping, also when the save failed for another reason; after a refused payload (unreadable, too large or stale) they hold what they held before the transport ran (the stored mapping unless nested params were posted too, so the conflict panel's current version must come from the stored row). The stored digest is memoized privately (`dcf_stored_dependencies_digest`, `dcf_memo` name `:dcf_stored_dependencies_digest`, keyed by the transport round, dropped on save); making it public changes the method lists pinned in `spec/lib/storage_limits_spec.rb` ('registration'), memoizing `stored_digest` under another `dcf_memo` name does not. A digest that differs from the stored one is not by itself proof of a posted payload (a GET /custom_fields/new with nested `value_dependencies` params assigns a mapping too); WP-22 exposes no 'applied in this request' flag.

**Related decisions and defects**: none

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Endpoint: `app/controllers/dcf_dependency_editor_controller.rb` (`DcfDependencyEditorController < ApplicationController`, `before_action :require_admin` as its only filter, `KIND_FORMATS`, one public action `values`) and one route, `get 'dcf_dependency_editor/values', to: 'dcf_dependency_editor#values', as: 'dcf_dependency_editor_values'`, after the audit route, without a format default. The optional `(.:format)` stays: `.json`, `.xml` and `?format=json` are API requests, so core ignores the session and answers 403 with an empty body (pinned; `format: false` was not used). Query parameters `custom_field_id`, `kind` and `type` (AD-7). Only String parameters are read, the id must match `DependencyRules::CANONICAL_ID`, and the field is read with `CustomField.find_by(id:)` after `require_admin`. An unknown or non-canonical id (`'06'`, `' 6'`, `'6abc'`, 25 digits, `id[]`, `id[a]`), a missing or unknown kind, a format outside the kind's family (`KIND_FORMATS`, equal to `DependencyRules::LIST_FAMILY`/`ENUM_FAMILY`) or a `type` other than `cf.type` (String compare, nothing constantized) gives 404 with exactly `{"error":"not_found"}`, never core's HTML 404 and never a 500 (`status: :not_found`, like the plugin API controller; the numeric rule of G2 is about 422). The 200 body is `{"id","name","kind","values"}`, `kind` from `DependencyRules.kind(cf)`, `values` = `DependencyEditorConfig.wire_values(DependencyRules.value_options(cf))`; a depending field of the family and a crafted self-parent are valid targets (editor design 12). Status matrix, pinned with a real login (`post '/login'`, no `User.current` stub): admin 200, also as an XHR with `Accept: application/json`; a logged-in non-admin 403 for every input before any lookup (spies on `CustomField.find_by` and `value_options`), also with an administrator API key header; anonymous Rails XHR (`Accept` `text/javascript` first) 401 with an empty body and `WWW-Authenticate: Basic` (core `format.js`); anonymous browser GET 302 to `/login`; anonymous XHR with `Accept: application/json` 403 without `WWW-Authenticate` (core `format.api` branch, so no browser login prompt); an API key header, a `key` parameter, HTTP Basic and an atom key never authenticate (REST API enabled), with or without `.json`; an admin session with `.json`, `.xml` or `?format=json` 403 without `WWW-Authenticate`. A `callback` parameter is ignored (body starts with `{"id":`), no CORS header, `Cache-Control` not public. Refused bodies are checked for the JSON key `"values"` and the value names, not the word values: the Rails 6.1 redirect body holds the requested path.
- Presenter: `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_editor_config.rb` (`RedmineDependingCustomFields::DependencyEditorConfig`, module_function, no state), required with `require_relative` from `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields.rb` (editor design 10; outside the Files list) so the parity spec sees `I18N` in a fresh process. `I18N = {'storageEstimate' => :text_dcf_storage_estimate}` (frozen, String JS key like `ClientConfig::I18N`, never merged with it; the three server-rendered texts have no JS key, editor design 9), `MAX_FORM_BYTES`, `wire_values`, `for_admin(field, view)` and `for_project(field:, parent:, view:, posted: nil, conflict: nil)`. Each call returns a new `Editor` (keyword Struct: `data_attributes`, `input_name`, `input_id`, `parent`, `required_warning?`, `required_warning_html`). The data Hash has a fixed insertion order (the row order of editor design 5.3), Symbol keys that Rails dasherizes, String scalars (`'0'`/`'1'` flags), Ruby Hashes and Arrays for the JSON attributes (encoded and escaped once by the tag helper) and nil for every absent attribute (never `false`).
- Admin parent: the raw `DependencyRules.parent_of` (gap 2): a blank, dangling, own, other-type or other-family pointer gives no `-parent-id`/`-parent-name`, `-parent-values` `[]`, `editor.parent` nil and the placeholder shown (QA-23). A parent in or below a stored cycle is rendered (the transport prunes against it too). Project mode renders only its `parent:` argument and never looks one up.
- Admin mapping, dirty and base: the in-memory mapping (`DependencyRules.mapping`/`defaults`, sanitized, not pruned, orphans kept); `-dirty="1"` only when `DependencyPayload.digest` of the in-memory mapping differs from `stored_digest` (an untouched saved field and a String default equal to a list of one are not dirty; a failed save with an applied payload, a copied mapping on /new and nested GET params are). `stored_digest` is called once per render (spy) and not memoized (no new `dcf_memo` name; `CustomFieldValidationPatch` unchanged, so the method lists of `spec/lib/storage_limits_spec.rb` hold). `-base` only for persisted records. Stale refusal (`dcf_dependencies_conflict` non-nil): `-conflict-mapping` is that Hash (`import` JSON null), the mapping comes from `attribute_in_database('format_store')` (sanitized; also when nested params changed the record in the same request), no dirty, `-base` the fresh stored digest; this case reads the stored YAML a second time.
- Project mode: `posted` and `conflict` count only as ok `DependencyPayload::Result` objects (anything else is ignored: the field's mapping, not dirty). An ok `posted` gives the posted mapping (sanitized, equal to the parser output) and `-dirty="1"` always (project design 2.1 and P2, not a digest compare; pinned with a posted mapping equal to the stored one); a `conflict` gives the field's mapping, no dirty and `-conflict-mapping` with the same four keys as admin, and wins over `posted`. `-always-submit="1"`; no `-base`, `-values-url`, `-*-source`, `-default-value-row` or `-allow-add-values`. The storage limit comes from the one private method `project_storage_limit(_field)` (`StorageLimits.column_limit('format_store')` until WP-31).
- Storage attributes: `StorageLimits.column_limit('format_store')` and, for admin list children only, `column_limit('possible_values')`, called with the column only (the spec seam); `-storage-base` is `StorageLimits.base_bytes(field)` (the WP-11 open point: plain `base_bytes`, no `storage_preview` variant; a String parent id posted on the admin form counts 2 bytes more, advisory only); `-storage-warn` `90` with any limit. `-warn-unsaved` is always present, `'0'` when the preference is `'0'`, else `'1'`. CSV separator and encoding are `I18n.t(:general_csv_separator)`/`(:general_csv_encoding)` in the current locale.
- Required warning: the rule of the current format partials, kept for WP-25: a required field, a parent that is its effective parent (`DependencyRules.effective_parent_id(field, {parent.id => parent})`, so none in or below a stored cycle, WP-18 review round 2) and an active parent value without links in the rendered mapping (inactive enumerations do not count, like `possible_values_options`). `required_warning_html` is always built: `content_tag(:span, sprite_icon('warning', text) or text, class: 'icon icon-warning')`.
- Partial `app/views/depending_custom_fields/_dependency_editor.html.erb` exactly as editor design 5.2 (plus one leading ERB comment, trimmed). The hidden input is the only named control and always blank (also after a failed save with request params and on a conflict re-render); no other id, no script, no `on*` or `style` attribute.
- Locale keys `text_dcf_editor_help`, `text_dcf_editor_noscript`, `text_dcf_editor_select_parent` and `text_dcf_storage_estimate` in en, de, fr and nl, byte for byte the registry texts, as flat keys under a comment line before the model errors block; no allowlist entry. A presenter example pins `text_dcf_storage_estimate` with interpolated values in the four locales.
- Fixtures: `spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` (type request) renders the bare partial (no surrounding form; WP-25 and WP-27 add page scenarios) in a view context of `CustomFieldsController` or `ProjectCustomFieldConfigurationController` with a test request, the admin as current user, locale en and `StorageLimits.column_limit` stubbed per scenario. Seven scenarios in `test/js/fixtures/editor/`: `admin_list` (required, Germany unlinked so the warning shows, orphan key Atlantis), `admin_enumeration` (multiple, inactive parent and child entries), `admin_new` (no parent: placeholder shown), `admin_conflict`, `project_list` (GET), `project_enumeration` (posted: dirty) and `mysql_limit` (65,535 for both columns, `-storage-base` replaced by `{{BYTES}}` with a targeted attribute rewrite; another example checks the exact value equals `base_bytes`). Records from `dcf_fixture_record` with fixed names; enumeration positions written with `update_columns` after creation (acts_as_positioned renumbers on create). Base files written on 7.0; 6.0 and 6.1 render the same; every scenario has a reviewed `.redmine-5.1.html` variant because 5.1 has no `sprite_icon` (an example checks that the variants differ only by the svg of the required warning). `dcf_js_normalize` (`spec/support/dcf_js_fixtures.rb`, outside the Files list) now also rewrites the `href` of svg `use` elements and `dcf_js_asset_url` accepts `#fragment` after the digest (the Propshaft sprite digest differs between 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0), with a unit example. The editor JSON attributes are not added to the shared guard's `JSON_ATTRIBUTES` (list values may be digit strings, `-max-form-bytes` holds integers): the enumeration keys of the enumeration scenarios are range-checked by the scenario checks, and the sequence-bump self check catches leaks.
- `test/js/fixtures/value_options.json`: `{"list": [...], "enumeration": [...]}` written by the editor fixture spec from `wire_values(value_options(cf))` (pretty JSON, final newline): a list with plain and tricky keys (`[x]`, `a]`, quotes, markup, `</script>`, `__proto__`, `constructor`, `10`, NFC and NFD), and an enumeration with two entries sharing a position (ordered by id), an inactive entry and an entry named like its id (no label). The same example checks endpoint (real admin login; both requests run before the presenter renders, which stub `User.current`) == presenter `-parent-values` == file for both; the request spec also checks endpoint == presenter for a list and an enumeration parent.
- JS consumer (outside the Files list, G1): `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js` (5 tests) decodes every editor fixture and variant with the WP-23 model (`ValueList.fromWire`, `State`, `isDigest`), checks one blank named control, the `data-dcf-editor` namespace and the mode rules, and `value_options.json`.
- Specs changed outside the new files, each with a WP-24 note: `spec/quality/locale_parity_spec.rb` (the positive example resolves `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` with `raise: true`; new negative example: the constant is defined, a missing `de.text_dcf_storage_estimate` is reported, and a stubbed map entry without a locale entry gives `<locale>.text_dcf_missing_key` for all four locales), `spec/quality/format_singleton_state_spec.rb` (scans `dependency_editor_config.rb`; the runtime example runs both modes and `wire_values` and checks the module keeps no instance variables), `spec/support/dcf_js_fixtures.rb` (normalizer). No characterization flip (`git diff 3a38a97 -- spec/characterization` empty); no other existing example changed.
- Not wired: no production view renders the partial (format partials, project view, head hook, `ClientConfig` and assets unchanged; `text_dependency_matrix_help` stays for WP-27); a request example checks that the admin edit page has no `data-dcf-editor` attribute and that only the controller and the partial under `app/` name the editor. No CHANGELOG, README or version change: the M3 list of section 3 has no WP-24 line (G9 N/A); no UN or PC row names WP-24 (the compatibility row 'New route GET /dcf_dependency_editor/values' is additive). `DependencyRules.value_options` unchanged (Ruby consumers keep the tuples; no `dcf_value_options_contract_spec.rb`).
- G6: the endpoint runs one field lookup plus one enumerations load (same count for 3 and 300 enumerations and for 3 and 2,000 list values, after a warm-up request); `for_admin` runs the parent lookup and one enumerations load per enumeration field, no `FieldIndex.load` (same count for 3 and 60 enumerations on parent and child and for 10 and 1,000 links, after the first `User#pref` read, which loads or creates the preference row). Editor data attributes measured (`:large`, `DCF PERF` lines): S1 (list child, 27 x 5,570) 147,252 bytes on every version, budget 300,000 (editor design 11: 0.2 to 0.3 MB); S2 (enumeration child, whose `-child-values` carries 5,570 labels) 559,859 bytes on 5.1, 6.0 and 6.1 and 571,107 on 7.0 (Rails 8.1 JSON escaping), budget 700,000.
- Evidence (HEAD 3a38a97 plus the uncommitted WP-24 tree, PostgreSQL): `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` gives 2,173 examples, 0 failures on 5.1 (seed 56719), 6.0 (seed 34024), 6.1 (seed 14408) and 7.0 (seed 38743) after review round 1 (2,072 before WP-24: 22 request, 49 presenter, 14 view and 14 fixture examples, one locale parity and one static scan example), js `# pass 434 # fail 0` (429 before, 5 in `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js`), lint `SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS` (the six new Ruby files without offenses); the new and changed spec files plus `spec/frontend` (187 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0; `spec/frontend` compare-clean with seeds 1 and 4242 on 7.0 and on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0; `git diff 3a38a97 -- spec/characterization` empty. A first matrix run failed two new examples on 5.1 only (Rails 6.1 writes the requested path into a redirect body, and collects every render of a view example in `rendered`); both specs were fixed, the code was not changed. Red first (7.0, before the production code): `spec/lib/dependency_editor_config_spec.rb` failed to load (`NameError`, no `DependencyEditorConfig`); the other new and changed files gave 82 examples, 50 failures (every endpoint, view and fixture example but the verb and guard ones, the static scan of `dependency_editor_config.rb`, the singleton runtime example and both locale parity examples); `npm test` failed 'one editor fixture per scenario (plus reviewed variants) and value_options.json' and 'value_options.json holds the compact wire shape of a list and an enumeration' (the three fixture loops passed with no files); the locale text example and the 'not wired' request example were added after the implementation, against behaviour that was already right. Mutation checks on 7.0, each restored and checked by sha256 and `git status`: (a) `before_action :require_admin` removed: 8 request examples fail (the controller, every non-admin and anonymous example, the format example); (b) `accept_api_auth :values` added: 'the controller runs require_admin first ...' and 'never authenticates an API key, HTTP Basic or a key parameter ...'; (c) `data-dcf-parent-values` instead of `data-dcf-editor-parent-values`: 21 examples (presenter name sets and namespace, endpoint == presenter, view escaping, all seven fixtures and the value_options example); (d) storage attributes without a column limit: 15 (presenter sets and 'renders none of the four without a column limit', six fixtures); (e) a label equal to the key emitted: 13 (wire_values, the endpoint list and enumeration examples, fixtures, value_options); (f) the hidden input echoing the mapping: 11 (four view examples, seven fixtures); a fixture with a non-blank input and a keyless wire entry fails two `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js` tests. G5 pass: the `security-review` skill could not run in this clone (no `origin/HEAD`); a manual pass over the diff (authorization before lookup, session-only authentication, input coercion, JSON-only output without JSONP or CORS, escaping, no echo, no parent lookup in project mode) found nothing to fix.
- Review round 1 (fixer, specs and docs only, no production change): the request spec now checks `WWW-Authenticate` (`Basic` on the anonymous Rails XHR 401, absent on the anonymous JSON XHR 403 and on the admin-session format 403); a presenter example renders an ok `posted` equal to the stored mapping and expects `-dirty="1"`; the value_options fixture example makes both endpoint requests before `render_admin` stubs `User.current`; the view example checks that neither the posted string nor its escaped form is rendered (the old `]}&quot;` check could not fail); the WP-25 and WP-27 hand-offs name the 'is rendered by no production view yet' example. Each was red first against the pre-fix specs on 7.0 under a mutation the old specs let through (98 examples, 0 failures each): a digest compare for the project dirty flag fails 'renders dirty 1 for a posted ok payload equal to the stored mapping'; a controller `head` that drops the header fails 'answers 401 with an empty body and a Basic challenge to an XHR'; one that adds it fails 'answers 403 without a challenge to an XHR that accepts JSON' and 'is not session-authenticated for .json, ?format=json or .xml'; the legend echoing `params[:custom_field][:dependencies_json]` fails 'keeps the hidden input blank after a failed save with a posted payload and with request params'. A probe raising in `values` when `User.current` is an RSpec stub failed the old value_options example and passes the new one. Mutations (a) to (f) rerun on 7.0 fail the same examples as above (8, 2, 21, 15, 13 and 11), and a fixture with a non-blank input still fails 'every fixture has one editor root, one blank named control and only data-dcf-editor names' in `npm test`.

**Definition of done**: the per-WP report of [`large_lists_quality_protocol.md`](large_lists_quality_protocol.md) (sections 1 to 9) with observed evidence for every applicable gate.

### WP-25: Admin dependency editor UI replaces the matrix (point 7)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | L | 7 | WP-18, WP-22, WP-23, WP-24 |

**Scope**

assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js and assets/stylesheets/dcf_dependency_editor.css; the head hook adds editor model JS, editor JS and CSS for CustomFieldsController and ProjectCustomFieldConfigurationController (class names compared as strings; the only inclusion path). Admin format partials reordered (default value row always rendered and toggled, url_pattern, parent select + cycle warning, hide_when_disabled, edit_tag_style, editor last); _dependencies_matrix and _default_dependencies and their CSS deleted. Editor: details/summary section per parent value (100 summaries per 'Show more', bodies built on open, 200 rows per page; UD-20), parent filter and 'only parent values without links', per-section child filter and views (all, linked, not linked here, not linked anywhere), check/uncheck all shown, counters, unlinked list, per-parent default select inside the section, inactive badge kept, orphan notice, live rebuild on parent select (values endpoint, stale responses ignored, dropped-count notice recomputed), possible-values textarea, multiple and required; hidden input '' until dirty; form switched to multipart after init (UD-16); pre-submit payload (4 MiB) and form-size checks; conflict panel on stale save (use mine, keep current, export mine; UD-17); format-switch confirm on /new; beforeunload only when dirty; inert fail-closed mode on bad data attributes. Project-mode features used by WP-27 (submit gate, always-submit, conflict) implemented and tested on the project fixture. Editor locale keys of the editor registry except import/export keys. README note: copy_from enumeration ids are not remapped (D6, SD-06).

**Consolidation amendments**

- Locale keys owned here: error_invalid_dependency_payload and error_dcf_dependencies_too_large_to_send (WP-27 reuses them, gap 8).
- Same-origin check before fetching data-dcf-editor-values-url (SP-15, gap 12).

**Files**

- assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js (new)
- assets/stylesheets/dcf_dependency_editor.css (new)
- `assets/stylesheets/depending_custom_fields.css`
- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/client_config.rb`
- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/hooks/view_layouts_base_html_head_hook.rb`
- `app/views/custom_fields/formats/_depending_list.html.erb`
- `app/views/custom_fields/formats/_depending_enumeration.html.erb`
- app/views/custom_fields/formats/_dependencies_matrix.html.erb (deleted)
- app/views/custom_fields/formats/_default_dependencies.html.erb (deleted)
- `config/locales/en.yml`
- `config/locales/de.yml`
- `config/locales/fr.yml`
- `config/locales/nl.yml`
- `README.md`
- test/js/dcf_dependency_editor.test.js (new)
- `spec/requests/admin_custom_field_dependencies_spec.rb`
- `spec/hooks/view_layouts_base_html_head_hook_spec.rb`
- spec/system/dcf_dependency_editor_spec.rb (new, opt-in)
- spec/characterization/admin_nested_params_spec.rb (listed flips)
- `CHANGELOG.md`

**Tests**

- jsdom: lazy sections, single named control, untouched admin form posts '', dirty flag, inert mode on truncated mapping (no write, no multipart switch), multipart switch, pre-check block/allow per enctype, conflict panel actions, filters and views, check all shown on unrendered rows, textarea rebuild, required warning live, parent change fetch + notice cleared on revert, format-switch cancel stops core handler, beforeunload only when dirty, 5,000 parents initial render bounded
- Request: multipart PUT with a 3 MB dependencies_json saved; urlencoded body above 4 MiB returns 404 (documents the pre-check)
- Head hook: editor assets once on the two controllers, absent on issue pages
- System (opt-in, 5.1 and 7.0): edit links, save, reload persisted; parent change live rebuild; stale conflict in two tabs; screenshots incl. icons
- Failed save re-render on the admin form: hidden input blank, posted mapping in data-dcf-editor-mapping, data-dcf-editor-dirty="1" (gap 1).
- jsdom: cross-origin data-dcf-editor-values-url: no fetch, load_failed notice (gap 12).
- test/js/dependency_editor_storage.test.js: warning with text_dcf_storage_estimate at or above data-dcf-editor-storage-warn percent of data-dcf-editor-storage-limit; no warning without the attribute; possible-values estimate checked against data-dcf-editor-values-limit (gap 12).

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0; npm test green; system specs green on 5.1 and 7.0
- Characterization flips listed (untick-all clears, brackets round-trip, inactive links kept)
- Locale parity green

**Compatibility notes**

The admin editor needs JavaScript (UD-16); without it saving leaves the mapping unchanged. Nested params still accepted. Theme overrides of the removed matrix partials or classes stop applying.

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; the matrix partials return and the admin form posts nested params again. Mappings normalized by editor saves meanwhile remain valid (same storage shape).

**Open input from WP-18**: `RedmineDependingCustomFields::ClientConfig` (`lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/client_config.rb`, module_function, no state) holds `META_NAME`, `I18N` (11 entries) and `payload`; `EDITOR_CONTROLLERS`, `editor_page?(controller)` and `editor_asset_tags(view)` are WP-25's (server design 2.4). The head hook (`hooks/view_layouts_base_html_head_hook.rb`) keeps its `context` argument and one `safe_join([...])` list (meta, runtime, wizard script, CSS): append the editor tags after the CSS when `ClientConfig.editor_page?(context[:controller])`. `spec/hooks/view_layouts_base_html_head_hook_spec.rb` checks the element list (`meta script script link`) and the needle order of the base assets; extend it with the editor assets on the two controllers and their absence elsewhere. The head fixtures (`spec/frontend/head_fixtures_spec.rb`, `test/js/fixtures/head/<locale>.html`) render the hook without a controller, so they stay unchanged. The matrix and defaults CSS rules are still in `assets/stylesheets/depending_custom_fields.css` (WP-18 removed only the dead spinner and `ul.cf-wizard` rules).

**Open input from WP-19**: `data-dcf-blank` (the id-less hidden sentinel before a managed single radio group or a required drop-down without a '' option) and `data-dcf-required-none` (the marked required '(none)' bulk option) are C1 contract names now; editor markup must not reuse them (frontend design 15).

**Open input from WP-21**: the server gives `:too_large` when the UTF-8 byte length of `dependencies_json` is greater than 4,194,304 (`DependencyPayload::MAX_BYTES`; exactly 4,194,304 passes), before any pre-scan or parsing; the client pre-check uses the same byte measure and the same strict `>`.

**Open input from WP-22**: an absent, empty or white-space `custom_field[dependencies_json]` keeps the stored mapping, so the editor posts `''` until dirty; a multipart text part reaches the transport (a 4,194,305-byte part gives the size message, not the unreadable one), a file part is refused as unreadable, and an urlencoded body over 4,194,304 bytes never reaches the model. Still to assert on the admin form (gap 1, not possible before the partial exists): after a save refused for another reason the hidden input is blank, `data-dcf-editor-mapping` holds the posted mapping and `data-dcf-editor-dirty="1"`; after a refused payload the editor shows the stored mapping. The `admin_nested_params_spec.rb` flips (untick-all clears, brackets round-trip, inactive links kept) are WP-25's. The `dcf_stale_dependencies` text promises that the editor shows both versions, which holds once the conflict panel built from `dcf_dependencies_conflict` exists. The CHANGELOG lines listed in WP-22's Delivered block go in with the form switch. Rails logs the posted parameter in full (core `filter_parameters` covers passwords and keys only); consider filtering `dependencies_json` when the editor posts it on every dirty save.

**Open input from WP-23**: add `dcf_dependency_editor_model.js` to the head hook first (model JS, editor JS, CSS); the first copy loaded is kept, so build every `ValueList` and `State` through `window.DcfDependencyEditorModel`. Decode `data-dcf-editor-parent-values`, `data-dcf-editor-child-values` and the values endpoint with `ValueList.fromWire` (a `TypeError` on a bad shape means inert, QA-24), the admin textarea with `ValueList.fromWire(listValuesFromText(value))`, the mapping and the conflict mapping with `new State(JSON.parse(...))` once they parsed to an object (`State` treats anything else as empty, so the shape check that makes the editor inert stays in the DOM layer, QA-24). Pass `multiple: attribute === '1'` (or the checkbox), `kind` from `data-dcf-editor-kind`, `base` from `data-dcf-editor-base` only when the attribute is present and `isDigest` holds (present but malformed: inert; absent on /new: serialize without `base`), never in project mode, and `parents` null after a failed values fetch. `stringify(serialize(...))` is the hidden input value and `utf8Bytes` the pre-check measure; S1-full with every link a default of a multiple field is 4,386,159 bytes, so the pre-check stops it before the server. Counters: `linkedCount`, `parentsWithoutLinks`, `unlinkedChildren`, `orphanCount` (orphans at init and the dropped count after a parent change), `conflictCounts`; filters use `tokens` and `matchesTokens` on `item.folded`.

**Open input from WP-24**: render the editor with `render 'depending_custom_fields/dependency_editor', editor: RedmineDependingCustomFields::DependencyEditorConfig.for_admin(@custom_field, self)` (positional). The partial always renders `p[data-dcf-editor-required-warning]` (hidden with `dcf-hidden` unless `required_warning?`, which keeps the rule of the current format partials, effective parent included), so the inline warning of the format partials can go. The values endpoint reads `custom_field_id`, `type` (`data-dcf-editor-field-type`) and `kind` (`data-dcf-editor-kind`) and answers `{"id","name","kind","values"}` or 404 `{"error":"not_found"}` (handle as `load_failed`); `data-dcf-editor-values-url` is a path without query or format, append the query with `URLSearchParams` and never use `.json` (403). The status of an expired session depends on the fetch headers: `X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest` with `Accept: application/json` gives 403 with an empty body (no prompt); a Rails-style XHR Accept (`text/javascript` first) gives 401 with `WWW-Authenticate: Basic`, which browsers may answer with a login prompt; pinned in `spec/requests/dcf_dependency_editor_values_spec.rb`. `data-dcf-editor-warn-unsaved` is always present, `'0'` or `'1'`. `data-dcf-editor-i18n` holds only `storageEstimate` (raw `%{label}`, `%{size}`, `%{limit}` and `%{percent}`); the two `%{label}` texts (`field_value_dependencies`, `field_possible_values`) are not in the map yet: add JS keys for them with the WP-25 keys, in the same commit as the locale entries, and update the key lists pinned by `spec/lib/dependency_editor_config_spec.rb` ('has its own frozen I18N map with only the WP-24 key'), `spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` and `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js`. The editor fixtures (`test/js/fixtures/editor/`, seven bare-partial scenarios) have a reviewed `.redmine-5.1.html` variant each (5.1 has no `sprite_icon`; the variants differ only in the required warning), so jsdom suites run every variant; regenerate with `DCF_WRITE_JS_FIXTURES=1 DCF_JS_FIXTURE_DIR=<repo>/test/js/fixtures .codex/test_plugin.sh 7.0 --suite rspec -- spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb`, then the same on 5.1, 6.0 and 6.1, and add page scenarios with the source controls (`#custom_field_parent_custom_field_id` and the others named by the `-source` attributes) when the DOM layer needs them. `test/js/fixtures/value_options.json` is `{"list": [...], "enumeration": [...]}` in the wire shape (an enumeration without a label, an inactive entry, tricky list keys). `spec/requests/dcf_dependency_editor_values_spec.rb` ('is rendered by no production view yet') pins the unwired state: replace it with a positive check on the admin edit page (`data-dcf-editor` present) and add the two format partials to its list of the files under `app/` that name the editor.

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Assets: `ClientConfig::EDITOR_CONTROLLERS` (frozen Strings `CustomFieldsController`, `ProjectCustomFieldConfigurationController`), `editor_page?(controller)` (String compare of `controller.class.name`; nil and subclasses give false, nothing constantized) and `editor_asset_tags(view)` (model JS, editor JS, editor CSS); module_function, no state, `I18N` still 11 entries. The head hook takes `context` and appends the three tags after the base CSS in its one `safe_join` only on those controllers (every action, so the /new AJAX re-render finds the scripts); without a controller the output is byte-identical (head fixtures unchanged), 0 queries.
- DOM layer `assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js` (IIFE, ES2017, load guard, `window.DcfDependencyEditor = {init(root), get(root)}`, one instance per root in a WeakMap, MutationObserver on `document.body`). `get(root)` returns the instance (`state`, `parents`, `children`, `config`, `dirty`, `inert`, `markDirty()`, `render()`, `notice(id, type, text, alert)`, `announce(text)`, `payload()`, `write()`) for WP-26 and WP-27. Every attribute is parsed and shape-checked in `readConfig`; any failure (mapping or conflict not an object, `fromWire` TypeError, malformed base, unknown mode or kind, max-payload or max-form not integers, a throwing `-source` selector, a missing model) leaves the editor inert: one `div.flash.error[role=alert]` before the hidden app element, no write, no multipart switch, no submit gate, no listener. An unreadable `data-dcf-editor-i18n` shows a fixed English sentence (no other text source exists). Text only through `textContent`, `createTextNode` and `setAttribute`; no selector is built from values; `%{name}` is replaced in one pass with a replacer function.
- Behaviour as editor design 5.4 to 5.11 and UD-16, UD-17, UD-20: 100 sections per page and 200 rows per page (Show more moves focus to the first new summary or checkbox), bodies built on `toggle` (capture phase, guarded) and removed on close; parent filter and 'only parent values without links' before paging; per-section child filter and view; check/uncheck all shown on every current match (rendered or not); Intl counters; unlinked list (first 200 on open); per-parent default (single select with '(no default)', multiple list box plus Clear, disabled without links); inactive badge; orphan notice and `data-dcf-ed-orphans` (shown while the rendered parent is selected); live parent change (same-origin check, `URLSearchParams` query, `X-Requested-With` and `Accept: application/json`, `credentials: 'same-origin'`, request token, `aria-busy`); textarea rebuild after 300 ms; multiple and required sources; hidden input `''` until dirty (admin) and always the JSON (project); writes 150 ms after a change and flushed on submit; pre-check (strict `>` on `utf8Bytes`, then the form estimate for the enctype at submit, the submitter included) with `preventDefault` and `stopPropagation`, so rails-ujs never disables Save; multipart switch after a successful admin init; project submit gate; one capture `change` listener on `document` for the format switch and one `beforeunload` listener (never `onbeforeunload`), `pageshow` with `persisted` resets the submitting flag.
- Decisions on the open questions: a malformed 200 body of the values endpoint is handled like a fetch failure (`load_failed`, parents null, the server prunes), not inert; the editor stays dirty after the parent is set back to the rendered one; the dropped-count notice shows only while the parent differs from the rendered one; the required warning follows the plain rule from the links and `#custom_field_is_required`, except that a warning the server hid although the rule held at render (no effective parent, stored cycle) stays hidden while that parent is selected; 'Use my version' closes the panel (a second use is impossible), 'Keep the current version' closes it, 'Export my version (CSV)' keeps it; the two storage estimates give one warning each, `%{percent}` floored; the possible-values estimate counts duplicates (its own split, not `listValuesFromText`).
- Conflict export: a minimal CSV builder in the DOM layer (UTF-8 BOM, CRLF, RFC 4180 quoting, `data-dcf-editor-csv-separator`, header parent name and field name, labels of the links over the current lists, no formula protection), downloaded through `Blob` and `URL.createObjectURL` (no download without them; the object URL and the link are released 1,000 ms after the click, as some engines start the download after `click()` returns); WP-26 replaces it with the model's `buildCsv`.
- Presenter (outside the Files list): `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` holds the 44 JS keys of editor design 9 for WP-24 and WP-25 (snake_case as section 9; `storageEstimate` kept), including the reused core and plugin keys and `label_value_dependencies` (`field_value_dependencies`) and `label_possible_values` (`field_possible_values`) for the two `%{label}` texts; `error_invalid_dependency_payload` has no JS key. `for_admin(field, view, index: nil)`: the format partials pass their `FieldIndex.load`, so the required warning reads the effective parent without one query per chain level (`custom_fields_admin_form_spec.rb` 'same number of queries for a parent chain of one and two depending levels' stays green); without an index the walk is unchanged (new records).
- Partials: both admin format partials compute the presenter first and render, in this order, the values (textarea or values link), `p[data-dcf-default-value-row]` (class `dcf-hidden` while `editor.parent` resolves), url_pattern, parent select, cycle warning, hide_when_disabled, edit_tag_style and the editor; the matrix, defaults and inline required warning renders are gone and `_dependencies_matrix` and `_default_dependencies` are deleted. The list row always exists; the enumeration row exists when core renders the select (persisted, active enumerations), the editor tolerates its absence.

### WP-26: CSV import and export of the mapping, add missing child values (point 8)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | L | 8 | WP-25 |

**Scope**

Pure model additions and editor panels (D7): parseRecords (RFC 4180, BOM stripped, CR/LF/CRLF, quoted newlines keep the start line, separator auto among tab ; , on the first 64 KB with manual override, 10,000,000 characters and 200,000 records caps, error list capped at 200), header auto/yes/no with a prominent warning listing skipped header cells, matching exact then exact NFC then unique case-insensitive NFC (enumerations by name incl. inactive, active+inactive duplicates and other duplicates reported as ambiguous, UD-19), planImport (merge default or replace, all-or-nothing, blocked when the resulting payload exceeds max-payload-bytes), preview table (first 20 pairs, counts, per-line errors), apply (snapshot, source = import, import {mode, rows}, dirty) and undo; nothing saved until the normal Save. 'Add missing child values' on the admin form for List (depending) children only: values with CR/LF rejected, trimmed and NFC-normalized, appended to #custom_field_possible_values with input/change events. File input decodes strict UTF-8 then falls back to general_csv_encoding or windows-1252 with a notice. Export buildCsv from editor state (UTF-8 BOM, CRLF, locale separator default, header with field names, quoting) as a Blob download; optional formula protection (prefix ') with the symmetric import option, both default off (UD-18). Import/export locale keys at parity.

**Files**

- `assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor_model.js`
- `assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js`
- `assets/stylesheets/dcf_dependency_editor.css`
- `config/locales/en.yml`
- `config/locales/de.yml`
- `config/locales/fr.yml`
- `config/locales/nl.yml`
- `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor_model.test.js`
- `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor.test.js`
- test/js/fixtures/payload/import_*.json (regenerated)
- `spec/system/dcf_dependency_editor_spec.rb`
- `CHANGELOG.md`

**Tests**

- RFC 4180 cases incl. multi-line quoted value start line, unterminated and bad quote; separator detection; header auto with cells reported; 200,001 records give too_many_lines
- Matching exact, NFC, NFD input vs NFC value, case-insensitive unique, ambiguous; active+inactive duplicate names ambiguous
- Add missing: collected once, CR/LF value is a line error, trimmed values appended and input/change fired
- Merge vs replace counts; undo restores state, source and import meta; apply blocked above the payload cap
- Export: BOM, CRLF, header names, quoting; protect and unprotect symmetric; export then import round-trips
- Payload contract spec posts the import goldens (admin)
- System: paste with add-missing, apply, save, values and links persisted
- jsdom: "Add missing child values" is not rendered on the project fixture nor for enumeration children (gap 12).

**Acceptance**

- npm test green; standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0; system spec green on 5.1 and 7.0
- Locale parity green with import/export keys

**Compatibility notes**

Client-side only; no new server endpoint. Admin mapping changes from imports stay unaudited (D6).

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; the import and export panels disappear, the editor stays.

**Open input from WP-23**: the model has no import code yet: add `parseRecords`, `planImport`, `applyImport`, `buildCsv` and the exact and NFC case-key Maps (`ValueList` holds `items`, `byKey`, `has`, `get`, `keys`). Apply sets `state.source = 'import'` and `state.importMeta = {mode, rows}`; `serialize` writes `import` only then (from `opts.importMeta` when given) and `State.clone` copies both for undo. Replace the stand-in `applyPairs` of `test/js/payload_golden.test.js` by `applyImport` and regenerate `import_merge.json` and `import_replace.json` with `DCF_WRITE_JS_FIXTURES=1 node --test test/js/payload_golden.test.js`; the contract spec already posts them with the same lists. Time plan + apply + serialize next to the 27 x 5,570 scale test of `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor_model.test.js` (shared generator).

**Open input from WP-24**: `data-dcf-editor-allow-add-values="1"` is rendered for admin list children only (absent on the `admin_enumeration`, `project_list` and `project_enumeration` fixtures); `data-dcf-editor-csv-separator` and `-csv-encoding` are `l(:general_csv_separator)` and `l(:general_csv_encoding)` of the current locale; `data-dcf-editor-max-payload-bytes` is `4194304`. Extend `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` (String JS keys, values locale key Symbols) in the same commit as the locale entries, update the key lists pinned by `spec/lib/dependency_editor_config_spec.rb`, `spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` and `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js`, and regenerate the editor fixtures (every file carries `data-dcf-editor-i18n`).

**Open input from WP-25**: `window.DcfDependencyEditor.get(root)` returns the instance (`state`, `parents`, `children`, `config`, `dirty`, `markDirty()`, `render()`, `notice(id, type, text, alert)`, `announce(text)`, `payload()`, `write()`); add the import and export panels inside `.dcf-dep-editor__body` after `details.dcf-dep-editor__unlinked`, unnamed controls only, and announce apply and undo through `announce`. The conflict panel's 'Export my version (CSV)' uses a minimal builder in the DOM layer (`csvText`: BOM, CRLF, RFC 4180 quoting, `data-dcf-editor-csv-separator`, header parent name and field name, labels over the current lists, no formula protection); replace it with the model's `buildCsv` so one builder remains. JS keys are snake_case as editor design 9; `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` has 44 entries (the WP-25 test lists in `spec/lib/dependency_editor_config_spec.rb`, `spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` (`config::I18N.keys`) and `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js` (`I18N_KEYS`)). `test/js/support/editor_page.js` wraps the generated fixtures in admin and project forms for jsdom.

**Related decisions and defects**: UD-18, UD-19

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Model (`assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor_model.js`, still ASCII, ES2017, no DOM and no output): `strip` (Ruby `strip` of NUL and ASCII white space by index; `listValuesFromText` and the DOM layer's possible-values estimate use it instead of the old `STRIP_RE`, which was quadratic on a long inner run of white space: `'a' + 80,000 spaces + 'b'` took 5.9 s), `parseRecords(text, sep, {fallback})` returning `{separator, records [{line, cells [a, b]}], first, errors (the first 200 by line), errorCount, lines}`, `ValueList.prototype.match(cell)` (the candidates of exact label, else NFC label, else NFC lowercase label; one index of three Maps per list, built on first use and kept on the list, never on the items), `planImport(text, state, parents, children, opts)`, `applyImport(state, plan)` and `buildCsv(state, parents, children, opts)`. Line errors and warnings are data (`{line, code}` plus `count`, `max` or `value`, the cell as read); the DOM layer words them with the `err_<code>` and `warn_inactive` keys. No existing export changed.
- Parser: one pass, single scan per record, cells kept up to 10 per record and counted beyond; a quoted cell is unescaped once (`split('""').join('"')`, the regex replace took 550 ms on 5,000,000 pairs); CR, LF and CRLF each end a record and count one line (`\n\r` counts two); one leading U+FEFF is dropped. A `"` opens a quote after leading white space (NUL, TAB unless it is the separator, VT, FF, space) and the same white space is tolerated after the closing quote; anything else there is `bad_quote` at that physical line, the rest of the line is skipped (quotes ignored) and parsing goes on (the bad-quoted cell stays in the record's cells with those before it, so a header skipped with `yes` still names it; review round 2); an unterminated quote is reported at the record's start line and ends parsing. Unquoted cells are stripped, quoted cells kept as they are; trailing empty cells (quoted or not) are dropped, a record with no cell left is a blank line; `columns` reports the count after that. Caps: above 10,000,000 characters (BOM included) only `too_large`, nothing parsed; record 200,001 (every non-blank record counts, a header and error lines included) gives only `too_many_lines` and no records. Separator auto: the real scan of the first 65,536 characters per candidate, at most 20 records, a record cut by the boundary ignored without an error, score = records with exactly two non-blank cells and no error (design 6.1); a tie goes to the candidate with more of those records without a quote inside an unquoted cell (`Paris,"Nord;Ost"` is `,`), then to the order tab, `;`, `,` (review fix: a quote inside an unquoted cell no longer removes a record from the score, so `12" pipe<TAB>Plumbing` alone detects TAB); nothing found: the locale separator when it is one of the three, else `,`.
- Plan: header `auto` skips the first record (the first non-blank one) unless it resolves as a pair, when a cell equals the current parent name or `parent` / the field name or `child` (NFC lowercase, after unprotect), or both cells are unknown values, which needs two non-blank cells (review round 2: a first record with one cell or a blank cell keeps its `columns` or `blank_value` error unless a name rule matches, so `Belgum` alone in replace mode no longer clears every link); a skipped first record with three or more cells gives no `columns` error; `auto` never takes a first record with a quote error for a header, while `yes` skips any first record but an unterminated quote (that record holds the rest of the input) and removes its error, a bad quote's error matched on the line of the quote (review fix: `yes` with a bad-quote first line used to block the import). Matching: exact, then NFC, then NFC lowercase (accents and special letters significant, never `fold`); several candidates at the first step that finds any give `ambiguous_*`, so enumerations with the same name (active and inactive included) are ambiguous and are matched by name only (UD-19: `#9200004` is an ordinary name); inactive matches give `inactive` warnings. Errors of parse and match merge by line (parent before child on a line), 200 kept, the rest counted; warnings are capped at 200 without a count line (the registry has no key for it). Pairs are deduplicated with a Map of Sets, never a joined key. `rows` is `pairs.length` (existing pairs counted, duplicates not). `removed` counts only links on the current lists (replace also drops orphans). `ok` (Apply) needs no error, a pair or replace mode with at least one record read (an empty input never clears the links, a header-only file does), and `estimatedPayloadBytes`, the UTF-8 length of `stringify(serialize(...))` of the applied clone with the editor's options (`kind`, `multiple`, admin `base`, the children extended by the new values), at most `maxPayloadBytes`. A merge that adds nothing is still applicable (design text).
- Add missing child values (`kind` `list` only; the model ignores the option for enumerations): an unknown child with CR or LF is `multiline_value`; otherwise the value is stripped, NFC-normalized, lone surrogates become U+FFFD (as the form posts them), a blank result is `blank_value`, the value is matched again (a quoted ` Brussels ` links Brussels) and new ones are collected once per NFC lowercase key in first-seen order. A value still holding U+0000 after the strip is `nul_value` (key `error_dcf_import_nul_value`, JS `err_nul_value`; review fix, refused rather than replaced as design 6.5 does for line breaks): the HTML parser turns NUL into U+FFFD when the textarea and the issue form options render again, so the stored value would not stay the link key and the next admin save would prune the link.
- Export: `buildCsv` returns the text with its one BOM (`download()` adds none, so the file starts with EF BB BF once), the header `[parent name or 'parent', field name or 'child']`, one row per link on the current lists in parent then child order with labels, CRLF after every row, separator `;`, `,` or TAB (anything else, such as core ro `.`, is `,`). A cell is quoted when it holds any of the three candidate separators (not only the chosen one, so auto detection cannot tie on field names or values such as `Nord;Ost`), `"`, CR or LF, or starts or ends with a character the import strips. Formula protection (UD-18, opt-in, both checkboxes unchecked on every load): protect adds one `'` to a cell whose first character after any leading apostrophes is `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, TAB or CR (header cells included); unprotect removes one `'` from such a cell, before header detection and matching. This widens design 6.3 and 6.7 to `^'*` so the two options are exact inverses for every value (`'=x` exports as `''=x` and comes back as `'=x`); values that do not start that way are never changed. The conflict panel's 'Export my version (CSV)' calls `buildCsv` with `protect: false` (csvCell and csvText are gone) and keeps its file name `dependency-mapping.csv`; the export panel's file is `<fold(field name), non [a-z0-9_.-] as _>_dependencies.csv`, else `dependencies.csv`.
- DOM layer (`assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js`): `details.dcf-dep-editor__panel.dcf-dep-editor__import` and `__export` after the unlinked list inside the body (hidden with it, and when there are no parent or child values), markup as editor design 6.8 with unnamed controls only, labels wrapping their controls, `type=button`, select values as tokens (`auto`, `semicolon`, `comma`, `tab`; the export select defaults to the token of `data-dcf-editor-csv-separator`, else comma). `readConfig` reads `-csv-encoding` and `-allow-add-values`; the add-missing checkbox exists only in admin mode for a list field with that attribute, without `-child-values` and with the values source found. Every import input change, every editor change (`markDirty`: links, defaults, check all shown, multiple, parent change, Use my version), a textarea rebuild and a values load clear the preview and disable Apply; Preview first flushes the 300 ms values debounce; Apply plans again on the current state and lists and applies nothing when that plan is not ok (it shows it instead). Apply snapshots `state.clone()`, the dirty flag and the possible values, appends new values (the textarea text without its trailing core strip characters, then LF-joined values; bubbling `input` and `change` built with the page's `Event`, the own 300 ms rebuild cancelled and the children rebuilt at once), applies, marks dirty, re-renders, closes the panel and shows `text_dcf_import_applied` with 'Undo import' in a `flash notice`, announced through the live region. `flash()` takes the sprite of core `notice_icon`: `checked` for `notice`, `warning` for `warning` and `error` (review round 2; it prepended `warning` for every type, so the WP-25 `loading` and `mine_loaded` notices change with it on 6.1 and 7.0; 5.1 and 6.0 keep the class background). Undo restores state, source, import meta, the textarea and the dirty flag (an untouched admin form posts `''` again); any later editor change removes the Undo button (no silent loss of that change); one level. Undo, Export and 'Export my version (CSV)' first flush the 300 ms values debounce like Preview and Apply (review fix), so a possible-values edit made just before the click counts: it withdraws Undo and the export uses the edited list (design 6.7, unsaved changes). File: at most 10,000,000 bytes checked on `file.size` before reading (the one `%{max}` of `error_dcf_import_too_large` for text and file), `arrayBuffer()`, strict UTF-8 with `ignoreBOM` (parseRecords drops the BOM once), else `TextDecoder(-csv-encoding)`, core CP932 and CP949 (ja, ko) under their WHATWG names `shift_jis` and `euc-kr` (review fix: both labels throw in `TextDecoder`), or windows-1252 when the label throws or is UTF-8, after a leading UTF-8 BOM; the decoder's name goes into `decoded_as`; a failed read or a missing `TextDecoder` is `err_unreadable`; a newer choice or Clear makes a pending read stale (token); the paste area is disabled while a file is used. UTF-16 files are not decoded specially. The current parent name (header detection and export header) comes from the values endpoint body `name`, else the selected option text, and is the rendered name again when the parent is set back. Cells, values and field names reach the DOM through `textContent` only; message values are cut at 200 characters plus U+2026; the static scan of the asset stays clean.
- Locales: the 48 WP-26 keys of registry 4.4 in en, de, fr and nl (47 plus `error_dcf_import_nul_value` of the review fix, registry row, totals and section 6 count updated), generated from the registry rows and checked against them by YAML load, as flat keys after the WP-25 block. Deviation: `text_dcf_import_separator_used` uses `%{name}`, not `%{separator}`: Ruby I18n reserves `separator` and raises `I18n::ReservedInterpolationKey` for any text containing it (i18n 1.14.8 and 1.15.2), so the registry text could not be rendered into `data-dcf-editor-i18n`; the registry row and editor design 9 are updated. `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` grows from 44 to 100 entries (the 48 keys under the JS names of editor design 9, then `export`, `preview`, `separator`, `comma`, `semicolon`, `yes`, `no`, `status`). fr `label_dcf_import_mode` and nl `label_dcf_tab_char` added to the parity allowlist (the registry's reviewed entries were not pre-listed). README unchanged (not in the Files list; see the WP-32 input).

### WP-27: Project dependency page on the shared editor with JSON transport and compact audit delta (point 9)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | L | 9 | WP-12, WP-21, WP-25, WP-26 |

**Scope**

edit_dependencies.html.erb keeps its multipart PATCH form and state_hash and renders the shared partial with DependencyEditorConfig.for_project; Save rendered disabled with data-dcf-editor-submit and enabled by the editor after init (no false success without JS, UD-16); global/shared scope banner via dcf_flash_box (notice_icon on 6.0+, classic on 5.1); notices instead of the editor when the parent is missing or not relevant. DependencyMappingService decodes dependencies_json with DependencyPayload.parse! inside perform! so every bad input (blank included) is an audited validation_failed; exposes parsed_payload; strict Set-based validation with aggregated offender summary (labels only in the audit summary, never in flash); shape_defaults! on the JSON path; legacy nested params still accepted when the key is absent, with a deprecation log (removed no earlier than 0.2.0). DependencyDelta v2: before {v, links, defaults, mapping_sha256}; after with counts, orphans_removed, defaults_changed, parents_changed, samples capped at 20 entries and 4,000 encoded bytes per list, labels cut to 80 encoded bytes, 3 labels per default, informational source and import (client-reported, SP-12). Controller prepare_dependencies reloads the field before computing state_hash (R9); 422 re-render passes posted: service.parsed_payload (only when it parsed) to DependencyEditorConfig.for_project; the hidden input stays blank and the payload is rendered in data-dcf-editor-mapping with data-dcf-editor-dirty="1"; 409 re-render passes the posted version as data-dcf-editor-conflict-mapping (parsed once, after authorization) so the same conflict panel as on the admin form appears (reconciliation: editor attribute instead of the project's data-dcf-editor-pending). Removes text_dependency_matrix_help. Payload goldens also posted to the project path.

**Consolidation amendments**

- Failed-save re-render: pass posted: service.parsed_payload to DependencyEditorConfig.for_project; the hidden input stays blank, the parsed payload is rendered in data-dcf-editor-mapping with data-dcf-editor-dirty="1"; no input_value, no data-dcf-editor-echo (gap 1).
- Uses DependencyRules.parent_of for text_dcf_parent_missing and text_dcf_parent_not_available (gap 2); consumes value_options as |key, label, active| tuples (gap 3); calls translate_error(e) (gap 4).
- Adds none of the dropped project keys: text_dcf_editor_pending_conflict, button_dcf_editor_pending_*, error_dcf_dependency_payload_too_large, text_dcf_orphan_entries, text_dcf_editor_requires_javascript (gap 8).
- Project-mode import is verified end to end with the WP-26 import panel (gap 14).

**Files**

- `app/views/project_custom_field_configuration/edit_dependencies.html.erb`
- `app/controllers/project_custom_field_configuration_controller.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_mapping_service.rb`
- app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_delta.rb (new)
- app/helpers/project_custom_field_configuration_helper.rb (dcf_flash_box)
- `config/locales/en.yml`
- `config/locales/de.yml`
- `config/locales/fr.yml`
- `config/locales/nl.yml`
- docs/specs/*.md (amendment A5)
- `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb`
- `spec/services/dcf_dependency_mapping_service_spec.rb`
- spec/services/dcf_dependency_delta_spec.rb (new)
- `spec/requests/dependency_payload_contract_spec.rb`
- `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor.test.js`
- spec/characterization/project_dependencies_spec.rb (listed flips)
- `CHANGELOG.md`

**Tests**

- JSON save redirects with one success row (source editor, mapping_sha256); brackets round-trip; {} clears; 4,200 links in one request
- Malformed, blank, unknown key, long number, oversize each give an audited 422 with the right message; parse called exactly once on 422 and 409 paths
- Unknown parent key 422 with offenders in the audit summary and the posted mapping in data-dcf-editor-mapping with data-dcf-editor-dirty="1" and a blank hidden input
- R9: storage 422 then corrected resubmit with the re-rendered state_hash gives 302, not 409
- 409 renders the DB state, a blank hidden input and data-dcf-editor-conflict-mapping
- Closed project 403 on the JSON path; Save disabled and noscript text without JS
- Delta v2 worst case at most 12,100 B and never shrunk by AuditPayload; import golden records source=import with mode and rows
- Legacy nested request without mapping params still clears (characterized)
- T-CASC-1: rename cascade keeps the child's unrelated, orphan and bracket keys byte-identical
- Failed save re-render: mapping attribute holds the posted mapping, dirty=1, hidden input blank (gap 1).
- Unauthorized PATCH update_dependencies with a malformed or 1M-digit payload: 403, an authorization_failed audit row, DependencyPayload.parse not called (SP-20b, gap 12).

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0; npm test green; system spec project editor on 5.1 and 7.0
- Characterization flips listed

**Compatibility notes**

Project nested params deprecated but accepted. Audit before/after of update_dependencies change shape (no view reads them; old rows unchanged). Managers need JavaScript to change mappings.

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; the project page returns to the matrix. Audit rows written in v2 stay valid JSON.

**Open input from WP-19**: `data-dcf-blank` (the id-less hidden sentinel before a managed single radio group or a required drop-down without a '' option) and `data-dcf-required-none` (the marked required '(none)' bulk option) are C1 contract names now; editor markup must not reuse them (frontend design 15).

**Open input from WP-21**: `parse!` raises `Invalid(:blank)` for nil and JSON white space only; NUL, U+00A0 and other content give `:invalid_json`. The codes are those of T-DEP-8 plus `:invalid_base` (a malformed `base` is rejected on the project path too, a well-formed one is ignored), so the T-DEP-8 loop iterates it as well; a JSON comment (`/` outside strings) is `:invalid_json`. Defaults come back in the submitted shape (a String, or an Array of Strings), so `shape_defaults!` takes both. `Invalid` is raised with `cause: nil` and its message is already the audit summary format. A spy on `DependencyPayload.parse` also sees `parse!` calls (it goes through `parse`).

**Open input from WP-22**: G5 is amended: only the 1,000,000-digit and the 20-digit integer are rejected before `JSON.parse` (pre-scan, `:number_too_long`); a negative integer passes the pre-scan and is rejected after it by the schema step (`:invalid_links`, or `:invalid_defaults` in a default), so the project-path spy example covers the two long integers only.

**Open input from WP-23**: add the project half to `spec/requests/dependency_payload_contract_spec.rb`; `golden(name)` (UTF-8), `with_base`, `expected_mapping(raw)` and `contract_fields(name)` (fields with the JS lists, enumerations at 9200001 and up) are methods in the describe. Every golden carries the well-formed placeholder base, which the project path ignores; the project editor calls `serialize` without `base`. Goldens hold no orphans, defaults only for linked keys, Strings for single fields (`clear`, `list_save`, `import_merge`, `tricky_values`) and Arrays for multiple ones (`enumeration_save`, `import_replace`); enumeration child ids are JSON integers (the parser gives Strings). `enumeration_save` (ids) and `tricky_values` (`'2'`, `'10'`) have array-index keys first in ascending order, not in parent order: the project save must not depend on the submitted key order.

**Open input from WP-24**: `DependencyEditorConfig.for_project(field:, parent:, view:, posted: nil, conflict: nil)` needs no change. It renders only the given `parent` (nil: no parent attributes, `data-dcf-editor-parent-values` `[]`), uses `posted` and `conflict` only when they are ok `DependencyPayload::Result` objects (anything else is ignored: the field's mapping, not dirty), sets `data-dcf-editor-dirty="1"` whenever an ok `posted` is rendered (also when it equals the stored mapping), and lets a `conflict` win over `posted` (the field's mapping, no dirty, `data-dcf-editor-conflict-mapping` with `value_dependencies`, `default_value_dependencies`, `source` and `import`, null when absent). Without `posted` the mapping is the in-memory one of `field`: pass the reloaded record on GET and 409. Input `dependencies_json`, id `dcf_dependencies_json`, `data-dcf-editor-always-submit="1"`, no `-base`, `-values-url` or `-source` attributes; the Save button with `data-dcf-editor-submit` is the view's. The required warning follows the admin rule with the given parent (required field, that parent is the effective parent, an active parent value without links). `spec/requests/dcf_dependency_editor_values_spec.rb` ('is rendered by no production view yet', or the positive admin check WP-25 makes of it) lists the files under `app/` that name the editor: add `app/views/project_custom_field_configuration/edit_dependencies.html.erb` and check that the project dependency page renders `data-dcf-editor`.

**Open input from WP-25**: the head hook already loads the editor model, script and stylesheet on every `ProjectCustomFieldConfigurationController` page (no `content_for`); the script is a no-op without `[data-dcf-editor]`. Project mode is implemented and tested on the project fixtures (`test/js/dcf_dependency_editor.test.js`): after a successful init every `[data-dcf-editor-submit]` in `root.closest('form')` is enabled (it stays disabled when the editor is inert), the hidden input holds the JSON from init on (no `base`), the conflict panel works as on the admin form, and the form must be server-rendered `multipart/form-data` (the editor switches only admin forms). WP-25 kept `.dcf-dependencies-matrix td.center` in `depending_custom_fields.css` and the locale key `text_dependency_matrix_help` for the project matrix: delete both with it. `CHANGELOG.md` already holds the canonical M3 lines with project-page parts (Added editor '... and on the project dependency page', Fixed '(admin form and project settings)', 'the project page disables Save', 'project audit rows count them', Removed `.dcf-dependencies-matrix` and `text_dependency_matrix_help`); the Deprecated line is still to add. `error_invalid_dependency_payload` and `error_dcf_dependencies_too_large_to_send` exist in the four locales.

**Open input from WP-26**: the import and export panels work in project mode without change (tested on `project_list` and `project_enumeration`, both variants): no add-missing checkbox (`data-dcf-editor-allow-add-values` is admin-only and the editor also requires admin mode and a values source), no request, and after Apply the always-written hidden input holds `source` `import` with `import` `{mode, rows}` (rows = distinct pairs read, existing ones included) and no `base`. The state keeps `source` `import` and the last import's meta after later manual edits (editor design 6.4 'last applied import'; such an edit withdraws Undo), so the compact audit delta of a save after an import and further ticks still reports the import; Undo, while offered, gives back the snapshot's `source` and `import`. Header detection and the export header use `data-dcf-editor-parent-name` (the project page has no live parent change) and `data-dcf-editor-field-name`; the export separator defaults to `data-dcf-editor-csv-separator`, the file fallback decoder to `data-dcf-editor-csv-encoding`. The import controls are unnamed, so the multipart project form posts only `dependencies_json` for them; core's `warnLeavingUnsaved` marks the paste textarea as changed like any textarea. The project system scenario 6 can import through `textarea[data-dcf-ed-import-text]`, `button[data-dcf-ed-import-preview]` and `button[data-dcf-ed-import-apply]`.

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Controller (`project_custom_field_configuration_controller.rb`): `update_dependencies` builds the service into a local before `call`; the rescue maps `:forbidden` to `deny_access`, `:not_found` to `render_404`, else `flash.now[:error] = translate_error(e)` and, for a 409, `prepare_dependencies(conflict: conflict_payload)`, otherwise `prepare_dependencies(posted: posted_payload(service))`, then the numeric 409 or 422. `dependency_params` copies `params[:dependencies_json]` raw when the key is present (JSON wins, nested params ignored), else the nested params through `unsafe_hash`. `prepare_dependencies(posted: nil, conflict: nil)` reloads the field first (R9), then `state_hash`, `DependencyRules.parent_of` and `@parent_available`; `posted_payload(service)` takes only an ok `parsed_payload`; `conflict_payload` parses a String only, once, on the 409 branch. The matrix ivars and `value_options` helpers are gone. The controller keeps one `l(` and `translate_error(:notice_dependencies_saved)` (spec S4), not `l(:notice_dependencies_saved)` of project design 3.2, and names neither the editor partial nor its presenter (the values spec scan).
- Service (`dependency_mapping_service.rb`): public `attr_reader :parsed_payload`; `perform!` in the design order (`parent_of`, relevance 404, `read_input!`, labels from `value_options` tuples, `Sanitizer`, `shape_defaults!` on the JSON path, `validate_mapping!`, `DependencyDelta` from the in-memory old mapping, one `field.save!`). `reject_payload!` raises with the `Invalid` message as summary, `size`/`limit` delimited and `cause: nil`. `legacy_input` logs the constant line `[DCF] update_dependencies received legacy nested params; send dependencies_json (deprecated in 0.1.0, removable no earlier than 0.2.0)` (the plugin's `[DCF]` prefix of WP-22 instead of project design 3.3's `[redmine_depending_custom_fields]`). `validate_mapping!(vd, dd, parent_keys, child_keys)` keeps the old call shape and goes through `DependencyRules.mapping_problems` (Sets; Arrays or the label Hashes), plus the several-defaults offenders that `shape_defaults!` keeps in `@several_defaults` (so `spec/lib/dependency_rules_spec.rb` 'is empty exactly when ... passes' stays unchanged; the `mapping_problems` comment now names the exception). Summary: only the groups with offenders, in the order unknown parent keys, unknown child values (`:unknown_default` counted here), defaults not linked (`parent / child` labels), several defaults on a single-value field; distinct offenders counted by key (two enumeration entries may share a label and then count twice), only the shown ones labelled, at most 5 shown, each cut to 40 characters ending in `...`, the whole cut to 1,000. Labels only from `labels(field)` and `labels(parent)`; anything else is shown raw (a foreign enumeration id stays an id). The service never assigns `dependencies_json`, never calls `prune_mapping` and never raises `ConfirmationRequired` (UD-23).
- Delta (`dependency_delta.rb`, new): keyword constructor of the design; `before`, `after` and `summary` built on call from counts computed in the constructor (no memo, no query, no class state). Old and new mappings go through `Sanitizer` first (mixed or nil stored values never raise). `mapping_sha256` is `DependencyPayload.digest` (decision on the open question: values as a set, a String default equals a list of one, same on json 2.6 and 2.21; it equals the design formula only when no value list holds a duplicate, always true on the JSON path; the legacy nested params may repeat a value, which the digest counts once, and project design 4 'Digest' is amended to say so; `before` equals the previous project row's `after` and `stored_digest` of the saved field). `links` counts distinct pairs, `defaults` the parent values with a default; `truncated` is always present and true only when a sample list is shorter than its full set (a cut label or a fourth default label does not set it). Samples are taken in value order (label position, unknown keys after the known ones, then by key) with `min_by(20)`, so they do not depend on the submitted key order; the byte cap counts one comma per entry (`ActiveSupport::JSON.encode`). Labels are scrubbed (`AuditPayload.text`), pre-cut to 80 characters and then cut by a binary search on the encoded length without the quotes (the limits prototype `label_cap.rb`). The summary has counts and the enumerated source only.
- View and helper: `edit_dependencies.html.erb` as project design 3.4 without the `content_for` (`dcf_config.css` is WP-28's; the head hook loads the editor assets); `hidden_field_tag :state_hash, id: nil`; Save `name: nil, disabled: true, data: { dcf_editor_submit: 1 }` inside the form. `dcf_flash_box(type, text)` as P-D12 (no id). `text_dcf_parent_not_available` names the parent through ERB (escaped once), as the 0.0.16 page did (decision on the open question: a manager of the field may see its configured parent; the project configuration pages already name every relevant field). A blank parent pointer keeps the 422 `error_format_unsupported` page (`require_dependency_capable` unchanged; T-DEPJ-25's 'blank' case is pinned as that 422). Closed projects render the page and refuse the PATCH with 403 (no read-only notice; unchanged scope).
- Locales: `text_dcf_parent_not_available` and `text_dcf_parent_missing` byte for byte from the registry after the WP-26 block in en, de, fr and nl; `text_dependency_matrix_help` deleted (189 flattened keys per locale, was 188); `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` unchanged. CSS: `.dcf-dependencies-matrix td.center` deleted. `git grep` finds `text_dependency_matrix_help` and `dcf-dependencies-matrix` only in docs and the CHANGELOG Removed line.
- CHANGELOG: the four WP-27 lines of section 3, word for word, in section 3 order: PC-57 'Changed: Project dependency audit rows store a compact summary ...' (its sort and reorder half becomes true with WP-29), PC-58 'Fixed: Correcting a failed dependency save ...', the Deprecated line, PC-67 'Changed: The project dependency page shows the global/shared field warning ...'. The project parts of the WP-25 lines are true now. README unchanged (not in the Files list; see the WP-32 input).
