- Tests added: `spec/services/dcf_dependency_delta_spec.rb` (new, 14), `spec/services/dcf_dependency_mapping_service_spec.rb` (+32: T-DEP-7, the T-DEP-8 loop of 13 codes plus blank and unreadable variants, T-DEP-10 to T-DEP-13, enumeration offenders by label and distinct offenders by key (review round 1), the legacy log, precedence, UD-23 and BC-02, parent and permission refusals before any parse, the hidden grandchild, T-CASC-1, placed here because `dcf_value_services_spec.rb` is not in the Files list), the WP-27 block of the request spec (49 examples: T-DEPJ-1 to T-DEPJ-25 except T-DEPJ-15, whose fixture path was superseded by the goldens of WP-23, plus the refusal matrix of SP-20b and an administrator's save on an active project, the hidden-field cases, de/fr/nl rendering and two G6 query-count invariance examples), the project half of the contract spec (6), `spec/system/dcf_project_dependency_editor_spec.rb` (new, 4, opt-in; outside the Files list because WP-27 acceptance asks for the project system scenarios: the served Save disabled and enabled after init, no unload prompt untouched, the failed save re-rendered dirty, a stale save across two windows with the conflict panel, a CSV import through the WP-26 panel saved and audited with `source` `import`; screenshots in `tmp/capybara/dcf/wp-27/`), and five project cases in `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor.test.js` (no editor asset changed). In a browser a hidden input's value attribute follows the editor's writes, so the blank served input of a failed save is pinned by the request specs, not the system spec. No new editor fixture scenario (`spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` is not in the Files list; the jsdom tests wrap the generated project fixtures).
- Evidence (HEAD b96945a plus the uncommitted WP-27 tree, PostgreSQL): `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` gives `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2289 examples, 0 failures suite=js exit=0 # pass 514 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 26767, 28504, 15002, 9391), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=136 base_offenses=103 head_offenses=70 fixed=25` (the new Ruby files without offenses), the working tree identical before and after the run (diff sha256); rspec grows from 2,194 to 2,289 examples (+95: 30 service, 45 request, 6 contract, 14 delta; 2,295 after review round 1: 32 service, 49 request) and `npm test` from 509 to 514. A first matrix failed two examples on 5.1 only: Rails 6.1 calls `JSON.parse` for its own session cookie on a later request, so the T-DEPJ-20 and T-DEPJ-21 spies now match calls with the posted payload (the short negative integer example shows that form sees a payload parse). `npm run check` `ok   ES2017` for every asset; core 7.0 stylelint config (16.10.0) on both plugin stylesheets exit 0. System (opt-in): `.codex/test_plugin.sh 5.1 --suite system` and `7.0 --suite system` 22 examples, 0 failures each (18 before, the 4 new ones included; screenshots `tmp/capybara/dcf/wp-27/`, the scope banner icon as class background on 5.1 and sprite on 7.0). The changed spec files (248 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0. `git diff b96945a -- spec/characterization` touches only `project_dependencies_spec.rb` (two hunks, the listed flips). G6 (`DCF PERF`, final matrix): delta worst case 11,058 bytes on every version after review round 1 builds every sample list at its byte maximum (10,452 before; budget 12,100), a 1,000,000-character label cut in 0.2 to 0.5 ms, a project PATCH with a 1,000,000-digit integer 66 to 96 ms and with a 20-digit integer 33 to 45 ms (bound 1 s); query counts equal for a save of 10 and 4,200 links and for the page of 10 and 1,000 parent values. Red first (7.0, before the production code): the subset (changed spec files, `spec/lib/dependency_rules_spec.rb`, `spec/characterization`, `spec/quality`) 624 examples, 87 failures (every new delta, service and contract example, the WP-27 request examples that need the new code, the amended T-UI-4/T-UI-7, the two flips, the values spec and T-AUD-10); the refusal examples and the five JS project cases passed already (they pin behaviour that exists; the JS side needed no change), so mutations show them. Mutations (each restored from a backup copy, sha256 and `git status` checked; 7.0 subsets or `node --test`): (a) a parse in `dependency_params` before the service: 18 failures (T-DEPJ-24, the parse counts of T-DEPJ-5/6/8/14/18, the six contract examples); (b) offenders interpolated into the flash text: 4 (T-DEP-10, T-DEPJ-6, the S4 key scan, a characterization 422 example); (c) sample caps 20,000 entries and 4,000,000 bytes: 5 (T-DEPJ-4, three delta examples, T-AUD-10); (d) the label cut by `byteslice`: 4 delta examples; (e) the posted payload echoed into the hidden input instead of `data-dcf-editor-mapping`: 7 (T-DEPJ-5 x3, T-DEPJ-6, T-DEPJ-8, T-DEPJ-14 and the GET query parameter example); (f) no reload in `prepare_dependencies`: 1 (T-DEPJ-14); (g) the nested params refused when `dependencies_json` is absent: 15 (T-DEP-1 to T-DEP-6, three characterization examples, T-DEPJ-9, the legacy request examples, T-SIZE-2); (h) Save rendered enabled: 1 (the GET example); JS: the project input written only when dirty: 4 tests, the gate enabling every `[data-dcf-editor-submit]` of the document: 1. G5 (security lens): no parse before authorization; a non-String payload is never read; offenders only in the audit summary; labels only of the field and its parent (a foreign enumeration id stays an id); the hidden-child, markup and XSS examples above; translate_error and ERB escape once.
- Review round 1 (fixer; HEAD b96945a plus the uncommitted tree, PostgreSQL): the offender summary counts and de-duplicates offenders by key and labels only the shown ones, so two enumeration entries that share a label count twice (semantics-r1-1); project design 4 'Digest' carries a WP-27 amendment (`mapping_sha256` is `DependencyPayload.digest`; the formula there holds only for duplicate-free value lists, which the legacy nested params need not give) and the delta decision above says so (contracts-r1-1); the WP-28 open input names the T-UI-4 assertion that pins the absence of `dcf_config` (contracts-r1-6). Tests: an enumeration pair whose 'defaults not linked' and 'several defaults' offenders are named by label, never by id (tests-r1-1); one unknown value under two parents and as a default counts once (tests-r1-2, inside the existing example); two same-named enumeration entries in both labelled groups (semantics-r1-1); the T-DEPJ-5 table gains a float, a null and a nesting deeper than 3 (tests-r1-3); T-DEPJ-20 sends one untimed warm-up request first (tests-r1-4); T-DEP-9 builds added and removed from 20 pairs of labels cut to exactly 80 encoded bytes (escaped and multibyte characters included, 3,361 bytes each) and defaults from 20 entries that fill the budget (3,999 bytes), and asserts those sizes from the class constants (tests-r1-5); an administrator saves on an active project (tests-r1-6); the UD-23 example checks the stored mapping, the import meta and the followed page instead of the redirect body (tests-r1-8). ### WP-28: Project values page: server-side search and pagination (point 9)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | L | 9 | WP-05, WP-12, WP-23 |

**Scope**

ValuesPage query object (UNPAGINATED_MAX 500, MAX_PER_PAGE 500 capping per_page_option, FILTER_MIN 25, page clamping, sortable? only when unfiltered and unpaginated, batch_scope page when filtered or paginated) (UD-21). ValueCollation with the same tables as the editor fold, asserted on the shared collation fixture (token AND substring match; empty token set is unfiltered). UsageCalculator.page_usage using one FieldIndex per page (2 grouped queries per page, exact counts). show.html.erb toolbar in core filter markup (fieldset, legend label_filter_plural, label, small Apply, Clear with icon-reload), filtered empty state with text_dcf_no_matches and Clear, drag handles and dcf_value_reorder.js only when sortable, info lines for filtered and large lists, pagination_links_full with per_page links; q and page preserved in rename/delete/enumeration forms, confirm panel and redirects; add_value jumps to the page of the new value; audit action selects only listed columns. Project rules moved from depending_custom_fields.css into new dcf_config.css included per view. Locale keys at parity.

**Consolidation amendments**

- Consumes value_options as |key, label, active| tuples (gap 3). Owns locale key text_dcf_no_matches unless WP-25 already landed it (then reuse; gap 8).
- Search box above 25 values (FILTER_MIN), pagination above 500 values (gap 11).

- app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/values_page.rb (new)
- app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/value_collation.rb (new)
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/usage_calculator.rb`
- `app/controllers/project_custom_field_configuration_controller.rb`

- `config/locales/nl.yml`

**Tests**

- 500 values keep drag markup; 520 values paginated without handles; page clamp; per_page capped at 500
- Token search case- and accent-insensitive ('lodz' finds 'Łódź'); combining-mark-only q unfiltered; no-match empty state
- Rename on page 3 with q redirects back; confirm panel carries q and page
- show_usage query count equal for per_page 25 and 100 (invariance); page_usage equals per-value methods
- Collation fixture read with encoding UTF-8 equals the JS fold results
- Assets: dcf_config.css on show, edit_dependencies, settings tab and audit; dcf_value_reorder.js only when sortable
- Opt-in :perf: search over 25,000 enumerations under 1 s

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0; npm test green
- Lists of 500 values or fewer render exactly as before apart from the search box above 25 values

**Compatibility notes**

Lists at or below 500 values keep drag-and-drop. Redirects now keep q and page. 'Show usage' counts become exact.

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; the values page renders every row again.

**Open input from WP-12 (SD-16)**: the confirmation panel (`_confirm_panel.html.erb`) names every dependent field from `UsageCalculator.affected_child_fields`, hidden and out-of-project ones included. Name a field only under the WP-12 rule of `BaseService#nameable_field?` (the edited field, an administrator, or used in the project and visible to the user) and count the others; a request spec shows a hidden child is not named.

**Open input from WP-23**: `ValueCollation.key` must give the keys of the model's `fold()`: the COMBINING ranges and SPECIAL of project design 2.7 plus U+03C2 to U+03C3 (final sigma), applied after `downcase`; white space is the Ruby `[[:space:]]` class (U+0009 to U+000D, U+0020, U+0085, U+00A0, U+1680, U+2000 to U+200A, U+2028, U+2029, U+202F, U+205F, U+3000), each run collapsed to one space and one leading or trailing space removed, neither `\s` nor `strip` (which also removes NUL). `test/js/fixtures/shared/collation_cases.json` is a JSON array of exactly 20 `[input, expected]` pairs, read with `encoding: 'UTF-8'`; mirror the 16 white space and sigma cases of `test/js/collation_parity.test.js` in `spec/lib/dcf_value_collation_spec.rb`. `tokens` of a query whose key is empty is `[]`.

**Open input from WP-27**: `edit_dependencies.html.erb` adds no `content_for` (the head hook loads the editor assets); add `dcf_config.css` there (T-ASSET-1) and move the remaining project rules out of `depending_custom_fields.css` (the matrix rule is gone). `dcf_flash_box(type, text)` exists in `ProjectCustomFieldConfigurationHelper` (core notice icon on 6.0 and later, no id); `show.html.erb` and `_confirm_panel.html.erb` still render their banners by hand (P-D12, project design 12), so adopt it there. T-UI-4 in `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb` currently asserts that the `edit_dependencies` body does not include `dcf_config`; flip it to `dcf_config.css` included exactly once (Changed by WP-28) together with T-ASSET-1. The audit page shows `changes_summary` only; `update_dependencies` rows now hold the compact delta, so the column select of project design 4 changes no output.

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- `ValueCollation` (`value_collation.rb`, new, `module_function`, frozen tables only): `key(label)` scrubs to valid UTF-8 (other encodings converted, invalid bytes U+FFFD, never raises), then NFKD, the five COMBINING ranges, `downcase`, SPECIAL with U+03C2 to U+03C3, each `[[:space:]]` run to one space and one edge space removed (no `\s`, no `strip`); ASCII skips NFKD and SPECIAL. `tokens(q)` is `[]` for nil or an empty key, else the key split on its spaces; `match?(key, tokens)` is the AND `include?` match (no Regexp from q); `text(value)` is the scrub step; `sort(items, desc: false) { |item| label }` is the stable sort for WP-29 (ties keep their order also for desc, a new Array).
- `ValuesPage` (`values_page.rb`, new): the design class plus `page`, `per_page`, `list_state` (q when filtered, the clamped page when above 1) and the class methods `coerce_query` (a String only, cut to 255 characters, no strip, per the WP-23 open input), `coerce_page` (Integer or String `to_i`, anything else 1), `coerce_per_page` (1..500, nil or junk 25), `state_for(q, page)` (the redirect state from posted params) and `page_for_index`. Rows are the `value_options` tuples with their index; labels are folded only when filtered, once each, q once; an unpaginated page shows every matching row (paginator per page 500); a paginated page clamps to 1..last_page (1 when nothing matches) before slicing; `Paginator.new(count, per, page)` without a controller.
- `UsageCalculator.page_usage(field, keys, project, index: FieldIndex.load)` returns `{key => PageUsage(here, other, refs)}`: two grouped `CustomValue` counts, own references in one pass over the field's stores, parent references from `DependencyRules.children_of(field, index:)`, no `USAGE_CAP`; the per-value methods are unchanged. The grouped counts are byte-exact through `grouped_value_counts(scope)` (review fix): MySQL and MariaDB compare the TEXT `custom_values.value` with the column collation, case- and accent-insensitive by default, so 'A' and 'a' fell into one group with an arbitrary label; there it groups by `CAST(custom_values.value AS BINARY)` and reads the binary keys back as UTF-8 (`Redmine::Database.mysql?`), elsewhere by `value`. The per-value methods keep the column collation and count such values together on MySQL and MariaDB, as before, so T-USE-7 holds for values that differ in more than case or accents. `nameable_child?(child, field, project, user)` is the one naming rule: `BaseService#nameable_field?` delegates to it (WP-28 close-out, base_service.rb outside the Files list; a spec compares both case by case).
- Controller: `prepare_show` builds `@values_page` after the reload with `per_page_option`, `@rows`, `@usage` (list family with `show_usage` only), `@default_options`; `@enumerations` and `@list_values` are gone. Every value operation redirects with `redirect_state(action)`: `ValuesPage.state_for(params[:q], params[:page])`, so the plain path stays byte-identical without q or page; `add_value_state` reloads the field and jumps to the page of the value (`value.to_s.strip` in the reloaded `value_options`, an enumeration's last row), only when paginated. `value_params` is unchanged (q and page never reach a service or the pending params). `audit` selects the listed columns (`AUDIT_COLUMNS`), counts on the unselected scope and takes its page through `coerce_page` (an Array page param raised `NoMethodError` in core's Paginator). One `l(` (S4).
- Views: `show.html.erb` links `dcf_config.css` always and `dcf_value_reorder.js`, handles and `_reorder_form` only when `sortable?`; the scope banner through `dcf_flash_box`; the toolbar partial above the box; info lines `p.info` (filtered count when filtered with a match; `text_dcf_drag_reorder_large` when paginated; `text_dcf_drag_reorder_filtered` when filtered, not paginated and more than one value); `text_no_values` when the field has no values (also with q), else `p.nodata` with `text_dcf_no_matches` and a plain Clear link; the full-mode enumeration form only when `batch_scope` is nil, read-only rows (Yes/No, delete link with the list state) on a filtered or paginated enumeration page until WP-30; pagination through `pagination_links_full` with a block that builds every link from the show path, q and `show_usage` (core links take the current route, a PATCH or DELETE path on a re-render); the list state is rendered once and repeated in the rename forms, the delete `button_to` params, the delete links, the default-value form and the Show usage link; rename rows have no ids (`old_value`, `state_hash`, `new_value` with `aria-label` `label_dcf_value`); the hidden `state_hash` of the add and default forms has no id; the remaining ids (`value`, `position`, `default_value`) are unique (project design 5; small lists keep their markup otherwise). `_values_toolbar.html.erb`: core filter markup in `div.dcf-values-toolbar`, the Clear link with `sprite_icon('reload', ...)` when available, plain text on 5.1 (no `dcf_icon`). `_list_state_fields.html.erb`: the local `state` (a Hash; `state.slice(:q)` for a form without the page), hidden q and page with `id: nil`. `_confirm_panel.html.erb`: `div.dcf-confirm-panel` around `dcf_flash_box(:warning, captured body)` (the helper has no class option and is outside the Files list), the list state fields once, the SD-16 rule. `dcf_config.css` (new) holds the moved rules unchanged plus the four design rules and is linked by show, edit_dependencies, the settings tab, the project audit page and the admin audit page; `depending_custom_fields.css` keeps the runtime and wizard rules.
- Decisions on the open questions: the SD-16 count uses no new key: `label_affected_child_fields: <count of all affected> (<nameable names>)` ('Label: %{count}' plus a parenthesised list, registry plural rule), the count alone when none is nameable; drop duplicate ids as project design 5 says (no spec pinned them); `text_dcf_drag_reorder_filtered` not on a paginated page (clearing the filter would not enable drag there); the Show usage link and the default-value form also keep q and page; redirects echo the posted page and the next GET clamps it (project design 14.11). Not changed: `DcfConfigAuditController#index` (outside the Files list; its view gained only `dcf_config.css`), the README (WP-32), `project_custom_field_configuration_redmine_integration_spec.md` section 11 (see the WP-32 input).
- Locales: the three registry texts after `text_dcf_parent_missing` in en, de, fr and nl (192 flattened keys per locale, was 189); `text_dcf_no_matches` reused. CHANGELOG: the two WP-28 lines of section 3, word for word (PC-59). SD-16 marked fixed in `large_lists_defects.md`.
- Specs changed on purpose: T-UI-4 (Changed by WP-28: `dcf_config.css` exactly once instead of no `dcf_config`). `spec/characterization` unchanged. Tests added: `spec/lib/dcf_value_collation_spec.rb` (new), `spec/services/dcf_values_page_spec.rb` (new, one opt-in `:perf` example), `spec/services/dcf_usage_calculator_spec.rb` (T-USE-7 and the SD-16 rule), the WP-28 block of the request spec (T-PAG-1 to T-PAG-7 and T-PAG-9 to T-PAG-13, T-ASSET-1 and -2, SD-16, banners, q escaping, de/fr rendering, registry texts, audit select).
- Evidence (HEAD ee1153d plus the uncommitted WP-28 tree, PostgreSQL): `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` gives `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2416 examples, 0 failures suite=js exit=0 # pass 514 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 40975, 36745, 9418, 18647), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=142 base_offenses=105 head_offenses=72 fixed=25` (the new Ruby files without offenses); the working tree identical before and after (diff sha256). rspec grows from 2,295 to 2,416 examples (+121: collation 49, values page 24 plus the opt-in `:perf` example, usage 7, request 41); `npm test` 514 unchanged (no JS change). Core 7.0 stylelint config (16.10.0) on the three plugin stylesheets exit 0. The four changed spec files (245 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0. `git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization` is empty. G6: `DCF_PERF_SPECS=1` search over 25,000 enumerations 0.414 s (q matching every row) and 0.268 s (no match) on 7.0, 0.397 s and 0.272 s on 5.1, bound 1 s (review fix: the example loads the 25,000 tuples through `value_options` before the timer, 0.432 s on 7.0 and 0.339 s on 5.1, printed apart, and stubs `value_options` with them, so it times only the fold, match and slice; the first run timed the load too, 0.866 s and 0.699 s on 7.0); query counts equal for per_page 25 and 100 and for the first and last page with `show_usage` (600 values), for 25 and 500 values with and without `show_usage`, and for 25 and 500 enumerations with `show_usage` changing nothing. Red first (7.0, before the production code): the subset (the four spec files, `spec/characterization`, `spec/quality`) 463 examples, 114 failures (every collation, values page and new usage example, T-UI-4 and 35 of the 41 new request examples); the six that passed pin unchanged behaviour (combining-mark q, `text_no_values` with q, the bare redirect, the partial reorder refusal, no usage query on an enumeration, enumeration query invariance); mutations (h) to (k) below show five of them, and the partial reorder refusal pins the unchanged `ReorderValuesService`. Mutations (7.0, the four spec files; each restored from a backup copy, sha256 and `git status` checked): (a) the panel naming every affected child: 3 failing examples (the three SD-16 request examples); (b) q echoed raw in the search box: 1 (G5 escaping); (c) per_page not capped: 3; (d) page not clamped: 3; (e) handles and the reorder script whenever more than one value: 3 (T-PAG-2, T-PAG-5, T-ASSET-2); (f) SPECIAL without the final sigma: 2, without the l with stroke: 6; (g) the rename redirect without q and page: 4 (T-PAG-6, T-PAG-7, the encoded q, the last-page removal); (h) `filtered?` by `query.present?`: 5; (i) usage on enumeration fields: 2; (j) the no-values branch dropped: 1; (k) page 1 kept in redirects: 5 (three existing redirect examples among them). None survived.
- Review fixes (r1): `page_usage` counts byte-exact on MySQL and MariaDB through `grouped_value_counts` (see the `UsageCalculator` item). `spec/services/dcf_usage_calculator_spec.rb` gains three examples: 'A', 'a', 'Muller' and 'Müller' counted apart on every database (red on MariaDB 10.11 with 7.0 before the fix: 'A' => [3, 1, 0], 'a' => [0, 0, 0]; green after on MariaDB with 5.1 and 7.0), and the MySQL branch and the default branch of `grouped_value_counts` with a stubbed `Redmine::Database.mysql?` (red on PostgreSQL before the fix). The audit select example finds the row query by its shape (LIMIT without COUNT, SCHEMA and cached statements skipped) and asserts only that a COUNT(*) ran: no absolute statement counts (G6). The opt-in `:perf` example times only the pure computation (see Evidence). The four WP-28 spec files (248 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0. `git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization` is empty. Mutations (a) to (g) on the final tree (7.0, each restored from a backup copy, sha256 and `git status` checked) failing examples: (a) 3, (b) 1, (c) 3, (d) 3, (e) 3, (f) 2 without the final sigma and 6 without the l with stroke, (g) 4, as in the first run; (l) `grouped_value_counts` without the MySQL branch: 1 on PostgreSQL (the stubbed MySQL branch), 2 on MariaDB with 7.0 (plus the case and accent example).

**Related decisions and defects**: UD-21, SD-16

**Definition of done**: the per-WP report of [`large_lists_quality_protocol.md`](large_lists_quality_protocol.md) (sections 1 to 9) with observed evidence for every applicable gate.

### WP-29: Sort A-Z / Z-A as an audited service (point 9)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | M | 9 | WP-28 |

**Scope**

Route PATCH custom_field_configuration/fields/:field_id/values/sort (sort_values), added to manage_project_custom_field_configuration in init.rb (granted automatically to holders), find_field and require_active_project filters. SortValuesService (BaseService, audit action sort_values): direction asc|desc else 422; stable sort with ValueCollation; list family saves possible_values; enumeration family assigns positions 1..N to all values incl. inactive (UD-24) with chunked CASE update_all of changed rows (no CustomFieldEnumeration update callbacks in 5.1 to 7.0); no-op still audited; before/after order_sha256. Buttons with JS confirm (UD-23) posting direction, state_hash and q. ReorderValuesService: index_by instead of O(N^2) find, order_sha256 in its audit (additive).

**Consolidation amendments**

- Calls translate_error(e) (gap 4).

**Files**

- `config/routes.rb`
- `init.rb`
- `app/controllers/project_custom_field_configuration_controller.rb`
- app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/sort_values_service.rb (new)
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/reorder_values_service.rb`
- `app/views/project_custom_field_configuration/_values_toolbar.html.erb`
- `config/locales/en.yml`
- `config/locales/de.yml`
- `config/locales/fr.yml`
- `config/locales/nl.yml`
- `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb`
- `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb`
- docs/specs/*.md (amendment A6)
- `CHANGELOG.md`

**Tests**

- List asc stable and accent/case-insensitive; desc keeps tie order; enumeration positions 1..N incl. inactive; no CustomValue rewrite; already sorted writes nothing and is audited with moved 0; chunked update with CHUNK stubbed to 2
- Invalid direction 422; stale state_hash 409; closed or archived project 403; user without permission 403
- order_sha256 before/after on sort and reorder audit rows

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0
- Permission matrix spec for the new action green

**Compatibility notes**

New action granted to existing holders of the permission (upgrade note). Reorder contract unchanged.

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; sort_values audit rows remain readable; the action disappears from the permission.

**Open input from WP-27**: the CHANGELOG line PC-57 ('Changed: Project dependency audit rows store a compact summary (counts, samples, SHA-256 of the mapping); sort and reorder audit rows store the SHA-256 of the order. ...') is in `## Unreleased (0.1.0)` word for word; its sort and reorder half becomes true with this WP (`order_sha256`). The project controller keeps one `l(` (inside `translate_error`) and every flash line `= translate_error(` (spec S4); call `translate_error(e)` with the error. UD-23 holds for dependency saves: `DependencyMappingService` never raises `ConfirmationRequired`.

**Open input from WP-28**: `_values_toolbar.html.erb` renders the filter form inside `div.dcf-values-toolbar` only when `@values_page.show_filter?` (above 25 values or with q); the sort buttons go into that div and its condition must also hold for `@values_page.total > 1`. Post `direction`, `state_hash` and only q: `list_state.slice(:q)` as `button_to` params (`show.html.erb` computes `list_state = @values_page.list_state`; `_list_state_fields` takes `state: list_state.slice(:q)`). `redirect_state(action)` in the controller returns `ValuesPage.state_for(params[:q], params[:page])` for every action but `add_value`; add a `sort_values` branch with `ValuesPage.state_for(params[:q], nil)` (drops the page). `ValueCollation.sort(items, desc: false) { |item| label }` is the stable sort (ties keep their order also for desc; returns a new Array); `@values_page.total` counts every value, inactive enumerations included. `value_params` does not permit q or page (they are read from params). `text_dcf_drag_reorder_large` already tells users to use the sort buttons.

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Route and permission: `patch 'custom_field_configuration/fields/:field_id/values/sort'` (`custom_field_configuration_sort_values`) next to the reorder route, PATCH only; `sort_values` next to `reorder_values` in the action list of `manage_project_custom_field_configuration` (`require: :member` and `read: true` unchanged, no new permission, no version bump). The em dashes of the touched comment lines in `init.rb`, the controller and the reorder service header became a colon or semicolon (repository dash rule).
- Controller: `sort_values` in the `find_field` and `require_active_project` lists (not `require_dependency_capable`), so a foreign field answers 404, an unsupported format 422 and a closed project 403 before the service, with no write and no row; an archived project is refused by core `authorize`. The action is `perform_value_operation(SortValuesService, action: :sort_values, notice: :notice_values_sorted)`: the flash goes through `translate_error`, the controller keeps one `l(` (S4). `redirect_state(:sort_values)` is `ValuesPage.state_for(params[:q], nil)` (a filtering q kept, the page dropped). `value_params` permits the scalar `:direction` only (`batch_scope` stays WP-30's; q, page and per_page are read from params).
- Service (`sort_values_service.rb`, new, `# frozen_string_literal: true`): `CHUNK = 500` is read when the method runs (`stub_const` works). The direction check runs in `perform!`, after the preamble (a stale hash answers 409 first), and accepts only a String equal to `asc` or `desc` (no strip, no case folding; a Symbol, an Array or a Hash is refused); the row stores the whitelisted value, never the posted one. List family: the raw `possible_values` (duplicates kept, not `value_options`, which uniqs) through `ValueCollation.sort`; one `field.save!` only when an index changed. Enumeration family: one `pluck(:id, :name, :position)` of every row, sorted in Ruby by `[position.to_i, id]` like `DependencyRules.enumerations_of`, stable sort by name, target positions 1..N; only changed rows are written, `each_slice(CHUNK)`, one `CustomFieldEnumeration.where(custom_field_id:, id:).update_all(['position = CASE id WHEN ? THEN ? ... ELSE position END', ...])` per slice (the bare model relation, integers only, `ELSE position` as a safety net); no `CustomField` save. Decision on 'moved': the list indices or enumeration rows whose stored value changes, so `moved` 0 holds exactly when nothing is written; gapped positions already in sorted order are renumbered and counted. Outcome and summary as project design 6, no `affected_child_field_ids`. Decision on the digest helper: the public class method `SortValuesService.order_sha256(keys)`, `Digest::SHA256.hexdigest(::JSON.generate(keys))` after `ValueCollation.text` on each key (never raises; a binary-encoded valid UTF-8 key digests like its UTF-8 form), called by `ReorderValuesService`; `base_service.rb` unchanged. Never raises `ConfirmationRequired` (UD-23). Decision on database errors: no new rescue; `ValueTooLong` keeps the `BaseService` mapping (a `save_failed` row without SQL), any other `StatementInvalid` propagates to core's 500 with the transaction rolled back and no row, as in every project service. Accepted as for the other services: the `state_hash` check runs in the preamble, outside the transaction, and the field row is not locked, so two concurrent writes can interleave; the next save of a page opened before either gets 409.
- Reorder (`reorder_values_service.rb`): `index_by` lookup; `update!` per row and the list `save!` kept; `before` `{order_sha256}` of the current order (`[position, id]`, inactive included) and `after.order_sha256` of the submitted order (additive; `order`, `truncated` and the summary unchanged; the validation compares the same set).
- Toolbar (`_values_toolbar.html.erb`; `show.html.erb` and the CSS unchanged, `.dcf-values-toolbar form { display: inline; }` exists): the div renders when `show_filter? || total > 1`, the filter form unchanged under `show_filter?`, the sort buttons whenever `total > 1` (decision on the visibility reading: project design 5 'when total > 1' and the design 14 table, so lists of 2 to 25 values get the buttons without a search box). Two `button_to` forms, A-Z then Z-A, `form_class: 'button_to dcf-values-sort'` (decision: core's `button_to` class kept, `dcf-values-sort` is the hook for WP-30; T-PAG-5 changed with a note), params `{ state_hash: @state_hash }.merge(@values_page.list_state.slice(:q))` plus `direction`, `data-confirm` `text_dcf_sort_confirm` with `count: @values_page.total`; no ids, no `html_safe`, no new query. Decision on closed projects: the buttons render there like every other write form; the PATCH answers 403.
- Locales: the four registry texts after `text_dcf_drag_reorder_filtered` in en, de, fr and nl (196 flattened keys per locale, was 192; no allowlist entry); `text_dcf_drag_reorder_large` unchanged. G8:

  | key | owner | en | de | fr | nl |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `notice_values_sorted` | WP-29 | Values sorted. | Werte sortiert. | Valeurs triées. | Waarden gesorteerd. |
  | `label_dcf_sort_az` | WP-29 | Sort A-Z | A-Z sortieren | Trier de A à Z | Sorteren A-Z |
  | `label_dcf_sort_za` | WP-29 | Sort Z-A | Z-A sortieren | Trier de Z à A | Sorteren Z-A |
  | `text_dcf_sort_confirm` | WP-29 | All values of this field (%{count}) will be sorted alphabetically and the current order will be replaced. Continue? | Alle Werte dieses Feldes (%{count}) werden alphabetisch sortiert und die aktuelle Reihenfolge wird ersetzt. Fortfahren? | Toutes les valeurs de ce champ (%{count}) seront triées par ordre alphabétique et l'ordre actuel sera remplacé. Continuer ? | Alle waarden van dit veld (%{count}) worden alfabetisch gesorteerd en de huidige volgorde wordt vervangen. Doorgaan? |

  Equal to the registry cell for cell (the request example reads the rows). Native-speaker review for de, fr and nl: machine-checked only, open.
- CHANGELOG: the section 3 Added line word for word after the Search line (PC-60). Decision on the upgrade note: a separate line 'Upgrade notes: Members whose role has the permission 'Manage custom fields' (manage_project_custom_field_configuration) can now sort values A-Z/Z-A; the new action is granted without a role change.' (UN-37 wording, naming the label the Roles page shows and the internal name, since the Added line keeps the section 3 name), added to the section 3 M3 list after the existing Upgrade-notes line and to `CHANGELOG.md` at the same place in the same edit. PC-57 unchanged and its sort and reorder half true now. Compatibility: PC-60 quotes both lines (with the inner quotes); the two 'Permission action list' rows corrected from '10 actions' to 11 actions plus `projects/settings`.
- Docs (amendment A6): `project_custom_field_configuration_review_log.md` 'Amendment A6' (search and pagination as built by WP-28, sort, reorder and the order digest by WP-29; page mode, the storage ceiling, the value cap and the parameter budget marked for WP-30 and WP-31, finalized by WP-32); operations §D and the new §J plus the error table, audit §4 and §5, UI §4 and §8, integration §1, §3 and §5, security model §15, permissions §4 and §9, functional §12, test plan §4 and §8, each marked '(Amendment A6, WP-29)' or '(Amendment A6, WP-28 and WP-29)'. Open inputs for WP-30, WP-31 and WP-32 written in their sections. README unchanged (not in the Files list; WP-32 input).
- Specs changed on purpose: T-PAG-5 (Changed by WP-29, was: 2 forms; the two sort `button_to` forms also carry q and no page, so 4). `spec/characterization` unchanged. Tests added: `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb` +40 (T-ORD-A three times, the sort-to-reorder digest chain, a foreign id order refused; T-SORT-10 to T-SORT-18 with ties, duplicates, gapped and tied positions, the chunk and last-chunk statements, integer-only SQL, the no-op cases, golden digests, invalid and binary keys, the refusals, UD-23, rollback of every chunk, `ValueTooLong` and other database errors, a hidden child, the CustomFieldEnumeration callback guard, G6 and one opt-in `:perf` example); `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb` +29 (route and permission, T-SORT-1 to T-SORT-6, UD-23, the audit page, the permission matrix, the service reached without the permission, 404/422/403, CSRF, the refusal order, the buttons and their params, the confirm count, escaping, de and fr, registry texts, G6, the redirect, a role-invisible child).
- Evidence (HEAD 7c85141 plus the uncommitted WP-29 tree, PostgreSQL): `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` gives `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2487 examples, 0 failures  suite=js exit=0 # pass 514 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 58639, 62203, 51642, 57515), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=145 base_offenses=110 head_offenses=77 fixed=25` (the new service file without offenses); the working tree identical before and after the run (diff sha256). rspec grows from 2,419 to 2,487 examples (+68: service 39 run by default plus the opt-in `:perf` example, request 29); `npm test` 514 unchanged (no JS change). The two changed spec files (294 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0. `git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization` is empty. G6: `DCF_PERF_SPECS=1` sorting 25,000 reversed enumerations issues exactly 50 UPDATE statements; the pure `ValueCollation.sort` of the 25,000 labels takes 0.074 s on 7.0 and 0.068 s on 5.1 (bound 1 s); UPDATE statements equal ceil(moved / CHUNK) (3 and 30 with CHUNK 10 for 30 and 300 values) and every other statement count is equal for both sizes; the show page has the same query count for 2 and 500 values. Red first (7.0, before the production code): the subset (the two spec files, `spec/characterization`, `spec/quality`) 512 examples, 67 failures (T-PAG-5 and every new example but two); the two that passed pin unchanged behaviour (a reorder with the ids of another field is refused; `CustomFieldEnumeration` has no update callback). After the code the same subset is 512 examples, 0 failures on 7.0 and on 5.1. Mutations (7.0, the two spec files and `spec/characterization`; each restored from a backup copy, sha256 and `git status` checked): (a) `sort_values` out of `require_active_project`: 2 failing examples (the closed-project 403 with no row, the refusal order); `authorize` skipped for `sort_values`: 2 (the permission matrix, the non-member 403 for any id); (b) desc as asc reversed: 3 (T-SORT-10, T-SORT-11, the enumeration ties); (c) inactive enumerations left out: 5 (T-SORT-2, T-SORT-12, the enumeration ties, the enumeration digest, the enumeration no-op); (d) a list sort that rewrites the CustomValue rows: 1 (T-SORT-13); (e) the no-op rolled back unaudited: 4 (the four no-op examples); (f) the last chunk skipped: 14 (among them T-SORT-17, the last-chunk example, T-SORT-12, T-SORT-2 and G6); (g) `order_sha256` dropped from the reorder row: 3 (T-ORD-A three times). None survived.
- Review fixes (light review): quality-r1-1, the hidden-child save-error request example now reaches `nameable_field?` (a storage error on the child itself, so `StorageLimits.violation_in` finds it) and has a visible-child counterpart: a hidden child gives the generic `error_save_failed` flash and a `validation_failed` / `error_dcf_values_too_large` row without its name, a visible child is named in `error_dcf_values_too_large` (sizes from `StorageLimits.delimited`: Redmine 5.1 en has no thousands delimiter). quality-r1-2, the anonymous branch of the T-SORT-4 matrix asserts the 302 to the login (G5) instead of 302, 401 or 403. Docs: the WP-31 open input from WP-29 (the ceiling never refuses a sort or reorder, P-D10), the WP-32 open input (2) (the permission label of the section 3 Added line), audit §5 and the test plan (the reorder digest chain holds only when no other value operation ran in between). Request spec +1 (30 examples for WP-29), rspec 2,488. Evidence: the matrix gives `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2488 examples, 0 failures  suite=js exit=0 # pass 514 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 57278, 1989, 47960, 50599), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=145 base_offenses=110 head_offenses=77 fixed=25`; the two changed spec files (295 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0. Red checks (each restored, sha256 checked): `raise error` without `nameable_field?` fails the hidden-child example; `nameable_field?` always false fails the visible-child example; a 403 for an anonymous `sort_values` fails the T-SORT-4 matrix. Mutations (a) to (g) rerun on 7.0: 8, 3, 5, 1, 4, 13 and 3 failing examples, none survived.

### WP-30: Page-scoped batch save for Key/Value list values (point 9)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | M | 9 | WP-28 |

**Scope**

UpdateEnumerationsService batch_scope: absent means today's full mode (unchanged), 'page' takes a non-empty subset of the field's ids, ignores positions, validates names and duplicate active names on the merged state, checks state_hash (409), clears a dangling default and audits scope page with row count; any other scope 422. Page-mode markup (hidden batch_scope, no position inputs, no handles, note text_dcf_page_scope_save, data-dcf-page-scope and data-dcf-warn-unsaved honoring the user preference). New assets/javascripts/dcf_values_page.js (ES2017, included only in page mode): dirty guard with beforeunload and confirm on pagination links, filter submit and sort buttons, cleared on submit.

**Consolidation amendments**

- Calls translate_error(e) (gap 4).

**Files**

- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/update_enumerations_service.rb`
- `app/controllers/project_custom_field_configuration_controller.rb`
- `app/views/project_custom_field_configuration/show.html.erb`
- assets/javascripts/dcf_values_page.js (new)
- `config/locales/en.yml`
- `config/locales/de.yml`
- `config/locales/fr.yml`
- `config/locales/nl.yml`
- `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb`
- `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb`
- test/js/values_page.test.js (new)
- `CHANGELOG.md`

**Tests**

- Subset applied; positions ignored; foreign id or empty subset rejected; rename onto an unsubmitted active name rejected; unknown scope rejected; audit scope/rows; deactivating the default clears it; full mode still rejects partial submits; stale state_hash 409
- Closed project 403 in page mode; redirect keeps page
- Page-mode form at per_page 500 has fewer than 4,096 inputs
- jsdom: dirty guard registers beforeunload, pagination confirm, cleared on submit, no attribute means no guard

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0; npm test green

**Compatibility notes**

Opt-in mode; full mode contract unchanged.

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; paginated enumeration pages lose their save form (WP-28 alone renders read-only rows) - revert WP-28 with it if needed.

**Open input from WP-28**: `show.html.erb` renders the full-mode enumeration form only when `@values_page.batch_scope` is nil; the `elsif enum_family` branch (filtered or paginated page) renders read-only rows (label, Yes/No, a delete link with `list_state`). Replace that branch with the page-mode form: hidden `batch_scope` `page`, `<%= state_fields %>` (the list state rendered once from `_list_state_fields`, so the `update_enumerations` redirect keeps q and page through `ValuesPage.state_for`), no `dcf-position` inputs and no handles. The header block includes `dcf_value_reorder` only when `sortable`; add `dcf_values_page` there when `@values_page.batch_scope == 'page'`. The filter form is `form.dcf-values-filter` and the pagination links are `span.pagination a`, built from the show path (also on a 422 or 409 re-render). `.dcf-page-scope-note` is in `dcf_config.css`. Page and per_page are already clamped (`ValuesPage::MAX_PER_PAGE` 500).

**Open input from WP-29**: the sort buttons are two `button_to` forms with the class `button_to dcf-values-sort` (select them as `form.dcf-values-sort`; their action ends in `/values/sort`) inside `div.dcf-values-toolbar`, after `form.dcf-values-filter`, outside the enumeration form and every table; they render whenever the field has more than one value, so also on filtered and paginated enumeration pages, and carry no ids. Each posts `_method` `patch`, `direction`, `state_hash` and a filtering `q` (never `page`); the submit is an `input[type=submit]` with rails-ujs `data-confirm` (`text_dcf_sort_confirm`), so the dirty guard must not ask twice (for example, ask on the form's `submit` or on a capturing click before rails-ujs and skip the second prompt). Do not reuse the class `dcf-values-sort` or the names `data-dcf-page-scope` and `data-dcf-warn-unsaved` elsewhere. T-PAG-5 counts `form.dcf-inline-form` plus `form.button_to` (4 on a filtered list since WP-29); a page-mode form without those classes leaves it unchanged. A sort that moves values changes `BaseService.state_hash`, so a page-mode form opened before it gets 409 on save (pinned by T-SORT-2 for `update_enumerations`).

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Service (`update_enumerations_service.rb`): `perform!` keeps the enumeration-family check first (a list field answers `error_format_unsupported` in page mode too), then `page_mode?`: `batch_scope` nil or a blank String is full mode, the old code path unchanged (exact id set, position sort, renumbering, summary and audit shape; `apply!(rows, positions: true)`); only the String `page` is page mode; anything else (`PAGE`, ` page`, `full`, a Symbol, an Array, a Hash) raises `error_reorder_mismatch` (422, one `validation_failed` row with summary nil; the posted value is never stored). It runs after the preamble, so a stale `state_hash` answers 409 first. Page mode (`perform_page!`): one `pluck(:id, :name, :active)` of every row in `[position, id]` order; the submitted keys must be a non-empty set of those ids compared as Strings (no `to_i`: `007`, `-5`, `7abc`, ` 7` and a duplicate `5` and `'5'` are refused), each with a Hash of attributes, else `error_reorder_mismatch`; only then are the submitted records loaded, scoped to the field (`field.enumerations.where(id:)`, never `CustomFieldEnumeration.find`), so an unknown id, another field's id and a hidden field's id get the same refusal (no oracle). Names are normalized and active cast as in full mode (a missing active means unticked); `validate_names!`; the duplicate check runs on the submitted rows plus every row not submitted (stored name stripped as a full-mode submit would be, stored active flag); `apply!(rows, positions: false)` writes only changed submitted rows through `enum.update!` (validations run; a 61-character name rolls the page back with `error_save_failed`) and never a position, so gapped or tied positions stay; `clear_dangling_default!` and `deactivated_usage` as in full mode. Audit: `after` keeps renamed, activated and deactivated (`DELTA_CAP`) plus `scope: 'page'` and `rows: n`; the summary reads `Saved n of N enumeration value(s) (page): <detail>` (N counts every row, inactive included; `no changes` still audited); `summary_detail` is shared with full mode, whose text is unchanged. `clear_dangling_default!` keeps the service's only `field.save!`; `DELTA_CAP` and `capped` stay here (WP-31). The comment lines with dashes are untouched.
- Decisions on the open questions: (1) the merged duplicate check is strict, as full mode: an active duplicate already stored among rows not submitted refuses every page save until one of the two is renamed or deactivated (pinned by a spec); (2) a non-scalar `batch_scope` over HTTP (`batch_scope[]=page`, `batch_scope[x]=page`) is refused with 422 instead of running full mode: `value_params` permits the scalar `:batch_scope` and sets the marker `:invalid` when permit dropped a posted one (never echoed); (3) a foreign id, an empty or missing `enumerations` and a non-Hash row use `error_reorder_mismatch`, as full mode T-ACT-4 and T-ACT-21; (4) the audit lists follow the stored `[position, id]` order; (5) no cap on the number of submitted rows beyond Rack (any subset of the field is valid); (6) full mode still raises `TypeError` (500, no row) for an Array `enumerations` (`enumerations[][name]=x`): full mode stays unchanged, page mode refuses it with 422; (7) blank (also white space only) is full mode, per project design 7; (8) a page-mode body that mixes valid rows with entries strong parameters drop (next to numeric keys `permit` keeps only numeric keys with Hash values, so `enumerations[x][name]=y`, `enumerations[7abc][name]=y`, `enumerations[7]=junk` and `enumerations[7][]=y` vanish) is refused whole with `error_reorder_mismatch` (422, one `validation_failed` row) instead of saving the rest: `value_params` marks `enumerations` `:invalid` in page mode when the permitted Hash has fewer entries than the posted one (review fix contracts-r1-1, never echoed); an unknown attribute inside a row is still ignored, and full mode keeps dropping such entries as before.
- Controller: only `value_params` (the scalar `:batch_scope`, the marker, the comment) and its helper `dropped_rows?` (decision 8, page mode only); `update_enumerations` is still `perform_value_operation` with `translate_error` (one `l(`); the redirect keeps a filtering q and a page above 1 through `redirect_state`; `find_field` and `require_active_project` answer 404 and 403 before the service, so a closed project writes nothing and no row.
- View: `page_mode = enum_family && batch_scope == 'page' && @rows.any?` (decision: the form and the script only where the form renders; filtered or paginated list pages and empty matches also have `batch_scope` page but get neither). The `elsif page_mode` branch replaces WP-28's read-only rows: `form_tag(..., method: :patch, class: 'dcf-enumerations-form', data: { dcf_page_scope: '1', dcf_warn_unsaved: text })` (decision: the class of the full-mode form; never `dcf-inline-form`, `button_to` or `dcf-values-sort`, so T-PAG-5 is unchanged), hidden `state_hash` and `batch_scope` without ids, `state_fields` once, per row the name input (no id, `aria-label` `label_dcf_value`), the hidden active `0` before the checkbox (no id, title and `aria-label` `field_active`) and the `delete_link` with the list state; `p.dcf-page-scope-note` inside the form above the one Save. No position input, handle or reorder form. `data-dcf-warn-unsaved` holds core `text_warn_on_leaving_unsaved` through the tag helper (escaped once) and is left out when `User.current.pref.warn_on_leaving_unsaved == '0'`. `dcf_values_page.js` is included next to `dcf_value_reorder.js` through `content_for :header_tags`, only in page mode (also on a 422 or 409 re-render).
- JS (`dcf_values_page.js`, new, ES2017 IIFE, `'use strict'`, no globals, no library): it starts on DOMContentLoaded, or at once when the DOM is ready, for the first `form[data-dcf-page-scope]` with a non-empty `data-dcf-warn-unsaved`, else it adds no listener; a `Symbol.for` marker on the form stops a second evaluation. Dirty means a text or checkbox control of that form differs from its markup default (reverting makes it clean; values the browser restored count). input and change on the form add the `beforeunload` listener (`window.addEventListener` only; the window property stays core's) while dirty and remove it when clean; it calls preventDefault and sets `returnValue`. Capture listeners on document, before rails-ujs's bubbling ones: a primary unmodified click on `span.pagination a` and a submit of `form.dcf-values-filter` ask `confirm(text)`; Cancel calls preventDefault and stopPropagation (rails-ujs never sees it, so Apply is not disabled), OK sets a leaving flag so the beforeunload of that navigation stays quiet; modified and middle clicks pass. Decision on the sort buttons ('must not ask twice'): a click on the submit of `form.dcf-values-sort` while dirty asks one combined question (the warn text, a blank line, the button's `data-confirm`), then hides `data-confirm` from rails-ujs for that click and puts it back on the next tick; Cancel submits nothing and keeps the guard; the sort text is never dropped (UD-23); a clean page leaves the question to rails-ujs. The page form's own submit sets the leaving flag; other forms do not (add value, default value, the form rails-ujs builds for a delete link), so the browser's own question follows there (decision: delete links, Back and the Clear links keep only the browser's question; project design 5 names pagination, filter and sort). The flag is one-shot (review fix quality-r1-3): it is set only while the beforeunload listener exists and the next beforeunload clears it, so an aborted navigation (Esc or Stop during a slow request, a dropped connection) leaves the guard armed for beforeunload, pagination, filter and sort; `pageshow` with `persisted` clears it too. It never calls `submit` or `requestSubmit`.
- Locales: `text_dcf_page_scope_save` after `text_dcf_sort_confirm` in en, de, fr and nl (197 flattened keys per locale, was 196; no allowlist entry); core `text_warn_on_leaving_unsaved` reused, no plugin copy. G8:

  | key | owner | en | de | fr | nl |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `text_dcf_page_scope_save` | WP-30 | Save applies to the values shown on this page only. | Speichern betrifft nur die auf dieser Seite angezeigten Werte. | L'enregistrement s'applique uniquement aux valeurs affichées sur cette page. | Opslaan geldt alleen voor de waarden die op deze pagina worden getoond. |

  Equal to the registry cell for cell (the request example reads the row). Native-speaker review for de, fr and nl: machine-checked only, open.
- CHANGELOG: the section 3 Added line word for word after the Sort line (PC-61); the restart sentence of the M3 Upgrade-notes line already covers the new asset (PC-65, UN-37), so no other line; no version bump.
- Not changed: `base_service.rb` (`state_hash` orders by position only; page mode no longer renumbers, so tied positions stay as they are), the README and Amendment A6 (WP-32 input), `spec/characterization`.
- Specs changed on purpose: the two WP-28 examples of 'enumeration pages before WP-30' (request spec), now 'enumeration pages in page mode (WP-30)', each with the note 'Changed by WP-30 (was: read-only rows)': they assert the page-mode form (hidden `batch_scope`, q or page, the name inputs) instead of its absence and keep the row counts, the list-state delete links and the drag notice. T-PAG-5, T-SORT-2, the WP-30 hook example and every T-ACT example are unchanged; `spec/characterization` is unchanged (`git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization` is empty). Tests added: `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb` +29 ('UpdateEnumerationsService page mode (WP-30)': T-ENP-1 to T-ENP-9, the scope table, the key table without an oracle, non-Hash rows, Integer and String keys, the merged duplicate cases including the strict legacy-duplicate decision, blank and 61-character names, `DELTA_CAP`, the full-mode audit shape, a hidden child, G6 invariance for 600 and 6,000 values and for 25 and 500 submitted rows); `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb` +28 ('page-scoped enumeration save (WP-30)': T-PAG-8, T-ASSET for `dcf_values_page.js`, the preference, escaping, the contract pin of the four JS hooks, de and fr, the registry row, T-ENP-R1 with q, page and the session per page, the 422 and 409 re-renders and the resubmit, 409 after a sort, T-ENP-R2, the permission and anonymous cases, CSRF, 404 and 422, the foreign, missing and hidden id refusal, malformed bodies, unknown and non-scalar scopes, T-ACT-21 over HTTP, mixed bodies (decision 8) and full mode unchanged for them, T-PARAM-1, G6 render invariance, a hidden child); `test/js/values_page.test.js` (new, 22 tests).
- Evidence (HEAD 9ffe66f plus the uncommitted WP-30 tree, PostgreSQL): `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` gives `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2540 examples, 0 failures  suite=js exit=0 # pass 534 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 62318, 53785, 43911, 30556), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=146 base_offenses=111 head_offenses=78 fixed=25` (the new and touched Ruby files without new offenses); the working tree identical before and after the run (diff sha256). rspec grows from 2,488 to 2,540 examples (+52: service 28, request 24); `npm test` from 514 to 534 (+20); `npm run check` prints `ok   ES2017 dcf_values_page.js`. The two changed spec files (347 examples) green with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 on 5.1 and 7.0. T-PARAM-1 measures the per page 500 form at no more than 3 x 500 + 8 named controls and the posted body below 4,096 pairs on every version. Red first (7.0, before the production code): the subset (the two spec files, `spec/characterization`, `spec/quality`) 565 examples, 44 failures (both rewritten examples and every new example but ten); the ten that passed pin behaviour the unchanged code already had (full mode refuses a foreign id, any partial payload and therefore every refused scope; the full-mode audit shape; a list field; a user without the permission; full mode, list and empty pages without page mode; non-members; 404 and 422; the foreign, missing and hidden id refusal), and mutations (a) and (d) below show they bite. jsdom red without the asset: 13 of 19 failing (the six passing pin the clean and no-guard cases), the later 'evaluated after the DOM is ready' test red as well. After the code the subset is 565 examples, 0 failures on 7.0 and on 5.1.

None survived.
- Review fixes (light review): contracts-r1-1, a page-mode body that mixes valid rows with entries `permit` drops is refused whole (decision 8, `value_params` and `dropped_rows?`); request examples for four mixed bodies (a non-numeric key, a scalar row, `<id>abc`, an Array row: 422, one `validation_failed` row each, nothing applied, an unknown attribute inside a row still saves) and for full mode keeping its old behaviour on such a body. quality-r1-1, a service G6 example compares 25 and 500 submitted rows on one 1,200-value field (same statements, one UPDATE; 500 untouched rows write nothing). quality-r1-2, two request G6 examples render the page-mode form with the same statement count for 520 and 5,200 values (page 2 and a filtered `q` page) and for 25 and 100 rows on one page. quality-r1-3, the JS leaving flag is one-shot and set only while the beforeunload listener exists; the Save test now expects a later pagination click to ask again, and two jsdom tests cover an aborted navigation after OK on pagination, filter and sort and after Save, and a Save of the clean form.
