### WP-31: Large-list safety on project pages: storage ceiling setting and value length cap

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | M | 9 | WP-11, WP-12, WP-27, WP-29, WP-30 |

**Scope**

ProjectStoragePolicy (plugin setting project_storage_ceiling_kib, default 2,048, minimum 64, in the settings partial; UD-22). CustomFieldValidationPatch adds a non-persisted dcf_storage_ceiling accessor and StorageLimits.effective_limit = min(column limit, max(ceiling, persisted bytes)) when the ceiling is set, so fields an admin made large can still shrink or keep their size from project pages but not grow. BaseService#save_field! sets and resets the ceiling and replaces every save! of a custom field in project services (add, rename, remove, reorder, set default, update enumerations default clearing, mapping, sort, cascade_parent_key!). New or renamed list values from project services capped at 255 characters (error_dcf_value_length). The project editor's storage attributes use the effective limit. README 'Performance notes' (YAML parse cost per request, load_ms in report_sizes, select sizes, transport caps and reverse proxy limits). Opt-in :perf specs.

**Consolidation amendments**

- Calls translate_error(e) (gap 4).

**Files**

- app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/project_storage_policy.rb (new)
- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/storage_limits.rb`
- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/patches/custom_field_validation_patch.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/base_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/add_value_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/rename_value_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/remove_value_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/reorder_values_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/set_default_value_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/update_enumerations_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_mapping_service.rb`
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/sort_values_service.rb`
- `app/views/settings/_dcf_project_config.html.erb`
- `init.rb`
- `config/locales/en.yml`
- `config/locales/de.yml`
- `config/locales/fr.yml`
- `config/locales/nl.yml`
- `README.md`
- spec/services/dcf_project_storage_policy_spec.rb (new)
- `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb`
- `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb`
- spec/lib/perf_spec.rb (new, tag :perf)
- `CHANGELOG.md`
- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_editor_config.rb`

**Tests**

- Setting parse, default, minimum, rescue
- On PostgreSQL with a stubbed small ceiling: add_value over it gives an audited 422; remove_value on a field already above the ceiling succeeds; ceiling uses max(ceiling, persisted bytes); save_field! resets the accessor even after an exception
- 256-character add or rename gives 422 error_dcf_value_length, audited; existing longer values untouched
- Admin form, API and import unaffected by the ceiling
- Opt-in :perf: validate_mapping 5,000 x 25,000 under 1 s, FieldIndex.load for 50 S1 fields under 50 ms
- DependencyEditorConfig.for_project storage limit equals ProjectStoragePolicy.format_store_limit(field) (gap 14).
- Plugin settings page: project_storage_ceiling_kib renders in en and de; a non-numeric POST falls back to 2,048 KiB (gap 12).

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0
- Grep shows no direct save! of a custom field left in project services

**Compatibility notes**

New refusal on PostgreSQL/SQLite for project-page writes that grow a field beyond the ceiling; configurable; admin and API untouched (upgrade note).

**Rollback**

Revert the PR; the setting key stays in plugin settings (harmless).

**Open input from WP-24**: the project-mode storage limit is the one private method `DependencyEditorConfig.project_storage_limit(_field)` (`StorageLimits.column_limit('format_store')` today); make it `ProjectStoragePolicy.format_store_limit(field)`. `data-dcf-editor-storage-base` (`StorageLimits.base_bytes(field)`) and `-storage-warn` follow the limit; `-values-limit` is never rendered in project mode. `spec/lib/dependency_editor_config_spec.rb` ('renders no values limit for an admin enumeration child and none in project mode') stubs `StorageLimits.column_limit` for the project mode and changes with it.

**Open input from WP-25**: the M3 Upgrade notes line in `CHANGELOG.md` already contains the sentence 'On PostgreSQL and SQLite, changes from project settings are limited by the new storage setting; the administration and the API are not.' (word for word from section 3), which becomes true with this WP. The editor's storage estimate reads `data-dcf-editor-storage-limit` in both modes, so the project mode follows `ProjectStoragePolicy.format_store_limit(field)` without a JS change.

**Open input from WP-27**: `DependencyMappingService#perform!` has one `field.save!` (after the delta, inside the `BaseService` transaction) for `save_field!(field)`; a storage refusal flows through the `BaseService` `RecordInvalid` mapping and the controller re-renders the posted mapping dirty with the reloaded `state_hash` (T-DEPJ-14 stubs `StorageLimits.column_limit('format_store')`). The controller and view pass no storage limit; `DependencyEditorConfig.project_storage_limit` stays the only place.

**Open input from WP-28**: after a successful `add_value` the controller finds the added list value by `value.to_s.strip` in the reloaded `DependencyRules.value_options` to redirect to its page (`add_value_state`); keep that in step if `AddValueService` changes its normalization.

**Open input from WP-29**: two `field.save!` calls to convert to `save_field!`: `SortValuesService#sort_list!` (the list branch, only when an index moved) and the list branch of `ReorderValuesService#perform!`. The enumeration branches save no `CustomField`: sort writes positions with `CustomFieldEnumeration.where(custom_field_id:, id:).update_all` in slices of `SortValuesService::CHUNK` and reorder calls `update!(position:)` per row, so the storage ceiling does not apply to them. A sort or reorder never grows a field (same values, new order), so the ceiling never refuses it (effective limit max(ceiling, persisted bytes), P-D10); only the column limit could, for a list stored above it; `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb` 'saves the field once ...' asserts exactly one UPDATE on `custom_fields` for a list sort. `base_service.rb` was not changed by WP-29.

**Open input from WP-30**: `UpdateEnumerationsService#clear_dangling_default!(deactivated_ids)` still holds the service's only `field.save!` (both modes call it after `apply!`); convert it to `save_field!(field)`, keeping the method and its call sites. `apply!(rows, positions:)` writes enumerations only, through `enum.update!` per changed row (no `CustomField` save, so the ceiling never applies there), and never a position in page mode (`positions: false`). `DELTA_CAP` and `capped` are still in this class. A page-mode or full-mode save never grows `possible_values`; clearing the default shrinks `default_value`.

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Policy (`project_storage_policy.rb`, new): `module_function`, no state (no instance or class variable, no memo; the setting is read on every call). `DEFAULT_CEILING_KIB` 2,048, `MIN_CEILING_KIB` 64, `MAX_CEILING_KIB` 1,048,576 (1 GiB; review round 1), `LIST_VALUE_MAX_CHARS` 255. `ceiling_bytes`: only a String or an Integer whose stripped text is ASCII digits counts (`' 128 '` and `'0128'` are 128 KiB); anything else (`abc`, `-5`, `1.5`, `+128`, full-width digits, an Array, a Hash, a Float, nil, a settings Hash without the key, a settings value that is not a Hash) gives 2,048 KiB; the value is clamped to 64 KiB to 1 GiB (`Integer#clamp`), so a 30-digit value gives 1,073,741,824 bytes, an Integer; any StandardError gives 2,097,152. `format_store_limit(field)` sets the accessor, returns `StorageLimits.effective_limit(field, 'format_store').first` and restores the previous value in `ensure`.
- Accessor: `attr_accessor :dcf_storage_ceiling` in `CustomFieldValidationPatch` (no new module, no callback, not in `CustomField.safe_attributes`, not in the API permit list). Decision: `save_field!` and `format_store_limit` restore the previous value instead of writing nil, so a nested call keeps the outer ceiling; outside them it is always nil (reload does not clear it, pinned).
- `StorageLimits`: new module functions `ceiling(record)`, `persisted_bytes(record, column)` (one serialize of `attribute_in_database`, never the assigned value; 0 for a new record), `stored_bytes(record, column)` (review round 1: the bytesize of the raw value of an attribute that was not assigned, `read_attribute_before_type_cast` and `<column>_came_from_user?`, nothing parsed or dumped; `persisted_bytes` otherwise) and `effective_limit(record, column, bytes = nil)`. Decision: the optional third argument takes the bytes `violations` already measured, so a save dumps each written column once below the ceiling and, above it, also parses and dumps the stored value once; without it (the editor) the stored size is `stored_bytes` unless the column limit applies, so the project dependency page parses no YAML for it. Without a ceiling the result is `[column_limit(column), 'column']` and nothing is measured; with one it is the smaller of the column limit and max(ceiling, persisted bytes), the column winning a tie, so MySQL TEXT (65,535 < 65,536) never measures. `column_limit` is still called on the module with the column only. `violations` skips a column without a column limit and without a ceiling (the WP-11 examples 'measures nothing' stay green); the ceiling applies only to a column the write itself changes (`check_column?`, computed once per column and passed to `writes_column?` as its new optional third argument; review round 1, UD-22), so a format_store that only the before_save of a depending format rewrites keeps the column limit alone (none on PostgreSQL and SQLite); then `writes_column?`, `preview_bytes`, `effective_limit` (or the column limit), and `writes_back?` only above the limit (its comment no longer says MySQL only). `Violation` gains `source`; `validate` passes `source: v.source`; `violation_in` rebuilds it. `usage` and `StorageReport` are unchanged. The audit summary keeps the WP-12 shape without the source (decision).
- `BaseService`: `save_field!(record)` as above around `record.save!` (the WP-12 rescue mapping and the existing `save!` stubs unchanged); `check_value_length!` (`OperationError(:error_dcf_value_length, interpolations: { max: 255 })`, summary `value: N characters exceeds 255`, lengths only, never the value or a field name); `cascade_parent_key!` saves each child through `save_field!`, so each child is measured against max(ceiling, its own stored bytes) and a role-invisible child keeps the generic `error_save_failed` (`nameable_field?`, unchanged); `DELTA_CAP` and `capped` moved here from `UpdateEnumerationsService` (project design 12; `UpdateEnumerationsService::DELTA_CAP` resolves by inheritance, the audit shape is unchanged); the header comment no longer names `save!`.
- Services: the ten custom field saves go through `save_field!` (add, rename, remove list and depending enumeration, reorder list, sort list when an index moved, set default, `clear_dangling_default!` with its two call sites, mapping, cascade). Enumeration writes are unchanged. Grep proof, also as a source-scan example: `grep -rn 'save!' app/services` prints only `base_service.rb: record.save!` inside `save_field!`; no `update_column`, `update_attribute`, `save(validate: false)` or `CustomField...update_all` exists.
- 255-character cap (list family only, `String#length`, so Unicode codepoints, not bytes or graphemes; decision). Add: blank, length, duplicate (an existing longer value equal to the posted one answers the length error; pinned). Rename: blank, length unless the new value equals the old one (decision: a no-op rename of a value stored before with more characters is allowed, nothing new is stored), not found, duplicate, then usage and `ConfirmationRequired`, so no confirmation panel and no write for a too-long value. Normalization stays `to_s.strip`, so `add_value_state` needs no change (the WP-28 page jump is pinned with a 255-character value with surrounding spaces). Values stored before stay untouched (remove, sort, reorder, set default, rename to a value of at most 255 characters; the review pins the rename order: length before not found and before duplicate). Enumeration names keep core's 60 characters (`error_save_failed`).
- Presenter: `project_storage_limit(field)` returns `ProjectStoragePolicy.format_store_limit(field)` (still the only place, private); project mode therefore renders `-storage-limit`, `-storage-base` and `-storage-warn` on every database and never `-values-limit`; `for_admin` is unchanged; the presenter sets no accessor itself (format singleton state scan unchanged). No JS change.
- Settings: `init.rb` default `'project_storage_ceiling_kib' => '2048'` (version stays 0.0.16). The partial adds a `<p>` with `label_tag` bound to `number_field_tag 'settings[project_storage_ceiling_kib]'` (`min` 64, `max` 1,048,576, `step` 1) and `em.info`; the two existing settings are unchanged (core replaces the whole Hash on POST). Decision: the field shows the value that applies (`ceiling_bytes / 1024`: `abc` shows 2048, `10` shows 64), so no stored value is echoed. A value above the maximum shows 1048576. The info text receives `kib: number_with_delimiter(2048)` in the current locale.
- Locales: `error_dcf_value_length`, `label_dcf_project_storage_ceiling` and `text_dcf_project_storage_ceiling_info` after `text_dcf_page_scope_save` in en, de, fr and nl (200 flattened keys per locale, was 197; no allowlist entry). Deviation: `%{kib}`, not `%{default}`: Ruby I18n reserves `default` (a `default:` option is a fallback, and a text containing `%{default}` raises `I18n::ReservedInterpolationKey`, i18n 1.14.8 and 1.15.2), as WP-26 found for `%{separator}`; the registry row, project design 11 and limits design 11 are updated. G8:

  | key | owner | en | de | fr | nl |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `error_dcf_value_length` | WP-31 | A value can have at most %{max} characters. | Ein Wert darf höchstens %{max} Zeichen lang sein. | Une valeur peut comporter au maximum %{max} caractères. | Een waarde mag maximaal %{max} tekens bevatten. |
  | `label_dcf_project_storage_ceiling` | WP-31 | Maximum stored size for changes made in project settings (KiB) | Maximale Speichergröße für Änderungen in den Projekteinstellungen (KiB) | Taille maximale enregistrée pour les modifications faites dans les paramètres du projet (Kio) | Maximale opgeslagen grootte voor wijzigingen in de projectinstellingen (KiB) |
  | `text_dcf_project_storage_ceiling_info` | WP-31 | Applies when the values or the dependency mapping of a custom field are changed from a project's settings. The custom field administration and the API are not limited by this setting. Default: %{kib} KiB. | Gilt, wenn die Werte oder die Abhängigkeitszuordnung eines benutzerdefinierten Feldes in den Einstellungen eines Projekts geändert werden. Die Verwaltung der benutzerdefinierten Felder und die API sind durch diese Einstellung nicht eingeschränkt. Standard: %{kib} KiB. | S'applique lorsque les valeurs ou le mappage des dépendances d'un champ personnalisé sont modifiés depuis les paramètres d'un projet. L'administration des champs personnalisés et l'API ne sont pas limitées par ce paramètre. Par défaut : %{kib} Kio. | Geldt wanneer de waarden of de afhankelijkheidskoppeling van een aangepast veld vanuit de instellingen van een project worden gewijzigd. Het beheer van aangepaste velden en de API worden door deze instelling niet beperkt. Standaard: %{kib} KiB. |

  Generated from the registry rows (with the placeholder rename) and checked by a request example in the four locales. Native-speaker review for de, fr and nl: machine-checked only, open.
- CHANGELOG: the two section 3 lines word for word, the Added line after the Page-scoped line and the Changed line after the Drag-and-drop line (the 26 M3 lines equal section 3 in order); the Upgrade-notes sentence on PostgreSQL and SQLite is unchanged and now true (PC-62, UN-61). No version bump.
- README: 'Performance notes' under 'MySQL / MariaDB and large lists' after 'Very large lists' (YAML parse cost per field and `load_ms`, select sizes and about 10,000 options, the 4 MiB JSON field and the 32 MiB Rack limit of Redmine's `config/boot.rb`, not the stale 4 MB of limits 7.6, nginx `client_max_body_size`, the extra YAML parse and dump of project saves above the setting and the raw stored size read by the project dependency page); 'Safety' gains the 255-character cap (a value stored before may be renamed to at most 255 characters); 'Settings' lists three settings with the ceiling (default, minimum, maximum 1 GiB, fallback, scope, refusal, MySQL column limit first, a mapping a save only rewrites is never refused).
- Decisions on the other open questions: (1) review round 1 (contracts-r1-2, UD-22): the ceiling applies only to a column the write changes, so a stored row whose YAML a save rewrites without changing it (for example a mapping stored before default_value_dependencies existed, which sort, reorder or set default on a depending field above the setting rewrite) is never refused by the ceiling; such a column keeps the column limit (MySQL TEXT) and the WP-11 `writes_back?` rule; the persisted bytes stay one serialize of `attribute_in_database` for a save, except that review round 2 measures a depending format_store against the stored row as a save writes it (`StorageLimits.baseline_bytes`) and makes the services assign only a mapping they change, so the rule also holds for remove, rename, the cascade and an unchanged mapping resubmit (below); (2) review round 1 (semantics-r1-1, contracts-r1-1, security-r1-1): the setting has an upper bound, `MAX_CEILING_KIB` 1,048,576 (1 GiB, above the largest PostgreSQL text value and SQLite's default string limit), so it still turns the ceiling off in practice while `data-dcf-editor-storage-limit` keeps at most 10 digits: the editor parses it with `INT_RE` (at most 16 digits) and goes inert above it, which an unbounded setting of 9,765,625,000,000 KiB or more did; the bound sits in the policy, not in a clamp of the presenter, because both callers (the save and the editor) read the same value; (3) no `maxlength` on the inputs (views outside the Files list; HTML counts UTF-16 units); (4) the project usage line stays MySQL-only; (5) the settings page request spec lives in the request spec of the Files list and stubs `Redmine::SudoMode.enabled?` (Redmine 7.0 asks for the password before admin pages).
- Editor fixtures (outside the Files list; review round 1, semantics-r1-3 and tests-r1-4, quality protocol G1 and C8): `spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` stubs `ProjectStoragePolicy.ceiling_bytes` to 2,097,152 in the two project scenarios (note 'Changed by WP-31') and checks the limit, the exact `StorageLimits.base_bytes` and the warn percent; the committed `project_list` and `project_enumeration` fixtures and their `.redmine-5.1` variants were regenerated (written on 7.0 and 5.1, compared on 6.0 and 6.1) and differ from ea14fec only by `data-dcf-editor-storage-limit="2097152" data-dcf-editor-storage-base="36" data-dcf-editor-storage-warn="90"`. The base is the real value, not a `{{BYTES}}` placeholder: the fixed fixture ids make it the same on every version and database, and the jsdom suites load these fixtures without a base override. `test/js/editor_fixtures.test.js` expects the three storage attributes on project fixtures; `test/js/dependency_editor_storage.test.js` adds the project estimate (one `storage_mapping` warning at 90 percent of 2 MiB, none one byte below, never `storage_values`, for `project_list` with ratio 1.2 and `project_enumeration` with 1.6) and the start with the largest rendered limit (1 GiB starts, 17 digits is inert with Save disabled).
- Not changed: the controller (`translate_error(e)` covers the new key; one `l(`), the views of the project pages, the JS assets (only `test/js` changed, above), `spec/characterization` (`git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization` is empty), Amendment A6 of the review log (WP-32 input).
- Specs changed on purpose, each with a 'Changed by WP-31' note: `spec/lib/storage_limits_spec.rb` (`Violation.members` gains `:source`; the exact `errors.details` of 'accepts ... at the limit and refuses one byte less', 'measures an unchanged format_store ...' and 'refuses any save of a possible_values ...' gain `source: 'column'`; `violation.to_h` gains it; the registration example's public methods gain `:dcf_storage_ceiling` and `:dcf_storage_ceiling=`), `spec/lib/storage_limits_scale_spec.rb` (`errors.details` gains `source`), `spec/services/dcf_storage_limit_services_spec.rb` (the audit marker case renames a 20,000-character value stored before instead of adding one, now refused by the cap), `spec/lib/dependency_editor_config_spec.rb` ('renders none of the four ...' keeps the admin roots and expects the three storage attributes with 2097152 on the project root; 'renders no values limit ... and none in project mode' compares with `format_store_limit`; `project_always` of '.for_project' includes the storage attributes, which also covers the two attribute lists of 'renders the project attributes of a list child' and 'renders an enumeration child'), `spec/requests/dcf_project_custom_field_configuration_spec.rb` (the WP-12 German flash example adds 255 instead of 1,500 characters; the `translate_error` key loop passes `max`), `spec/frontend/editor_fixtures_spec.rb` (above).

Mutations (7.0 or `npm test`, each restored from a backup copy, sha256 and `git status` checked): (a) 6, (b) 16, (c) 10 (here: the ceiling falls back to `ceiling_bytes` when the accessor is nil), (d) 3, (e) 9, (f) 2, (g) 5, (h) 6 failing examples, the same kinds as above plus the review examples; (i) no maximum: 6 (the parse rows 1048577 and 9765625000000, the Integer row, the 30-digit row, the presenter largest-setting example, the clamp request example); (j) the ceiling on every written column (the round 0 rule): 3 (the depending-list sort and reorder service example, both changed-column unit examples); (k) the editor measuring with `persisted_bytes`: 2 (the raw-size unit example, 'runs no query and no YAML parse or dump'); (l) the rename length check after the not-found and duplicate checks: 1 (the rename order pin); (m) the editor ignoring `data-dcf-editor-storage-limit` in project mode (JS): 2 jsdom tests (the project estimate, the 1 GiB start).
- Review round 2 (fixer; findings contracts-r2-1, semantics-r2-1, tests-r2-1, tests-r2-2, nits contracts-r2-2 and tests-r2-4): a mapping stored without `default_value_dependencies` (an old version's row) was refused above the setting by a remove or rename of an unlinked value, a depending enumeration remove, an unchanged mapping resubmit, a short linked remove and a parent cascade (a role-invisible child gave `error_save_failed`), because the services wrote `{}` over the missing key and the baseline was the raw stored row while the measured write always carries that key. Fixed twice, as both findings ask: (a) `BaseService#assign_mapping(record, name, mapping)` (private) assigns a rewritten mapping only when its content changed, a missing one read as {}; `rewrite_own_list_deps!`, `prune_own_deps!`, `cascade_parent_key!` (each store on its own) and `DependencyMappingService#perform!` use it, so a write that changes no mapping leaves format_store unchanged and keeps the column limit alone (decision (1)); (b) `StorageLimits.baseline_bytes(record, column)` replaces `persisted_bytes` in the save branch of `effective_limit`: `persisted_bytes` for every other column and format; for a depending format_store one read of `attribute_in_database`, one serialize, and the larger of that and the dump of `sanitized_store` (both mappings sanitized as the before_save writes them, a missing one as {}; the parent id stays as stored, a save never makes it longer), dumped only when the sanitized Hash differs (compared as Hashes), so a normal row costs no extra dump. Decision: the helper sits in StorageLimits next to `MAPPING_KEYS`, not in `DependingFormatMethods` (its public methods are pinned: 'has exactly the nine shared public methods'); the editor keeps the raw stored size (`stored_bytes`, no parse on a page view, round 1), so for such a legacy row its advisory limit is up to that key's bytes (31 bytes of YAML) below what a save allows. README: the parse cost bullet says 'mappings of about 0.5 to 2 MB of YAML' (the research run: 99.9 ms at 490 KB, 211.9 ms at 2.06 MB), the save bullet adds the second dump for such a mapping, Settings adds that a change to such a mapping is measured against the size a save of the stored mapping writes; limits design 4.11 and project design 2.4 name `baseline_bytes`. Specs: `spec/services/dcf_value_services_spec.rb` +5 (a depending list above the setting without stored defaults: unlinked remove and rename, linked remove and same-length rename, and a growing rename refused with the sanitized stored size as limit; format_store unchanged by an unlinked remove, an unlinked rename, an unchanged JSON resubmit and an unlinked depending enumeration remove; a depending enumeration's unlinked and linked remove; a parent's same-length rename and remove cascading into a visible and a role-invisible child without stored defaults; the G6 query pair above the setting on depending lists) and the cascade example changed (tests-r2-1, note 'Changed by the WP-31 review round 2': the ceiling between the parent's grown list and the child's stored mapping, the refusal's limit is the child's stored size, a parent remove prunes the child, title without 'never the edited field'); `spec/services/dcf_project_storage_policy_spec.rb` +1 (`effective_limit` on a normal and a legacy row: the stored size as a save writes it, one dump then three) and the T-SIZE-7 policy-raise half changed (tests-r2-4: a previous 7 is restored); the request spec +1 (an unchanged mapping resubmit of such a child above a ceiling of 1 byte answers 302 with a success row). Red first against the unchanged production code, 7.0 and 5.1 alike: the three files 471 examples, 5 failures (the list, enumeration and cascade examples, the `effective_limit` example, the request example; the enumeration and list removes and the cascade rename raised `OperationError`, the request answered 422, `effective_limit` gave 72 for 103); the format_store example, written after that run, failed on 7.0 and 5.1 with the three start files swapped in (`[true, true, true, true]`); the changed cascade, G6 and policy-raise examples passed there (test strength, shown by mutations (b), (q), (q2) and (a2)). Green: the WP spec files with spec/characterization and spec/quality 1,039 examples, 0 failures on 7.0. `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` gives `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2667 examples, 0 failures  suite=js exit=0 # pass 538 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 44746, 63250, 3995, 45592), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=153 base_offenses=115 head_offenses=82 fixed=25` (the new and touched Ruby lines without offenses; the compat scan first flagged the word 'normalizes' in a comment, reworded); the working tree was identical before and after the run (diff sha256); rspec 2,660 to 2,667, npm 538 unchanged. The nine WP-31 spec files with seeds 1, 4242 and 31337: 630 examples, 0 failures on 5.1 and on 7.0 (six runs). System specs: 22 examples, 0 failures on 5.1 and on 7.0. MariaDB 10.11 (the 5.1 and 7.0 copies): the storage subset 663 examples, 4 failures on 7.0 and 3 on 5.1, the full suite 2,678 examples, 4 pending (all UD-38), 6 failures on 7.0 and 5 on 5.1, the same examples as rounds 0 and 1 (present at ea14fec, listed in the WP-32 input); every round 2 example passes there. `git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization` is empty; the grep proof still prints only `base_service.rb:213: record.save!`. Mutations (7.0, the nine WP-31 spec files plus `spec/requests/custom_fields_storage_limit_spec.rb`, 652 examples, each restored from a backup copy, sha256 and `git status` checked): (a) no `ensure`: 6; (a2) the `ensure` writes nil: 2 (the nested call, the changed policy-raise half); (b) the ceiling alone: 22 (round 1's plus the five legacy and cascade examples, the changed cascade example, the G6 pair above the setting); (c) the ceiling when the accessor is nil: 10; (d) `SortValuesService` with `field.save!`: 3; (e) 256 characters accepted: 9; (f) bytes instead of characters: 2; (g) below 64 KiB accepted: 5; (h) a role-invisible child named: 6; (n) `assign_mapping` always assigns: 1 (the format_store example); (o) the baseline back to `persisted_bytes`: 4 (the list, enumeration and cascade examples, the `effective_limit` example); (p) the sanitized store always dumped: 1 (the `effective_limit` dump count); (q) `persisted_bytes` reading the row with a query: 1 and (q2) `baseline_bytes` doing so: 1 (both the G6 pair above the setting). The PostgreSQL and MariaDB checkouts' `log/test.log` files were truncated after the runs.

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | S | 7, 8, 9 | WP-21, WP-22, WP-23, WP-24, WP-25, WP-26, WP-27, WP-28, WP-29, WP-30, WP-31 |

**Scope**

Version 0.1.0 in init.rb and the release tag (UD-01 resolved: one release for all milestones). The CHANGELOG 'Unreleased (0.1.0)' section becomes '0.1.0', grouped by Added, Changed, Fixed, Deprecated, Removed, Security, API and Upgrade notes, and gets the M3 lines (PC-45..PC-65, PC-67, PC-68 incl. Deprecated project nested params with removal no earlier than 0.2.0 and Removed matrix partials/CSS/key); README (editor, import/export, project pages, reverse proxy note, no-JS behaviour); docs/specs amendments A5 and A6 finalized (operations, audit, UI, integration, security model, permissions, functional spec, test plan); upgrade-notes register checked completely (quality section 9).

**Files**

- `init.rb`
- `CHANGELOG.md`
- `README.md`
- `docs/specs/*.md`

**Tests**

- Full matrix rspec + lint, npm test, full opt-in system set on 5.1 and 7.0, manual MariaDB run

**Acceptance**

- One SHA green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1, 7.0 with system specs on 5.1 and 7.0
- Every upgrade-notes register entry has its CHANGELOG line, and the 0.1.0 section contains all lines of M1, M2 and M3 (PC-04, PC-06..PC-69)

**Compatibility notes**

Minor release: admin and project editors need JavaScript; admin form multipart; project nested params deprecated.

**Rollback**

Do not tag; revert the version bump.

'keeps the stored order of ties and sorts desc on the same keys' (both): the fixture's `where(name: ['b', 'ä'])` also matched 'A' under the case- and accent-insensitive collation; the sort is pure Ruby; fix: the rows picked in Ruby, plus a pre-sort state assertion. 'updates only the changed rows, one CASE statement per CHUNK' (7.0 only): Rails 7.2 and later quote bound integers on MySQL (`WHEN '12' THEN '1'`); fix: optional quotes in the match and the id scan, and the scan must find both ids of each slice. 'saves a multipart PUT with a 3 MB dependencies_json (UD-16)' (both): TEXT cannot hold the mapping; fix: a variant that, below the size, expects the storage error (exact message with the column limit) and an unchanged stored mapping, so the 3 MB multipart body still reaches the model. 'costs the same queries for 10 and 5,000 Key/Value links' (both): 71,387 bytes above TEXT; fix: the large mapping keeps as many parents as fit the measured limit (5,000 links without a limit, at least 2,500 asserted), the count equality and the differential of 2 unchanged. Each variant and guard carries a 'Changed by WP-32' note and is decided on the owner's mandate (to be confirmed by the owner); none uses a `:mysql` tag, which would drop the example from PostgreSQL. UD-38 gains a note listing the six guard sites (status unchanged).

### Owner follow-up of 2026-11-02 (WP-33 to WP-36)

After WP-32 the owner reviewed the mandate decisions (decisions register, 'Confirmed by the owner on 2026-11-02') and asked for four changes, the SD-17 fix, the CSV import on an empty list, a Security line for SD-16 and an integration test with redmine_extended_api. They ship in 0.1.0 with the rest: every new or changed CHANGELOG line goes into the section 3 M3 list and into 'Unreleased (0.1.0)' word for word, with its PC row in `large_lists_compatibility.md` and, when it changes behaviour for existing users, its UN row in `large_lists_quality_protocol.md` section 9. No version bump, no tag, nothing to main (owner rule). Every WP ends with the per-WP report and its Delivered block.

### WP-33: Exact value matching on MySQL/MariaDB (SD-17), CSV import on an empty list, Security line, Extended API integration

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | M | 8, 9 | WP-26, WP-28, WP-32 |

**Scope**

- SD-17: on MySQL and MariaDB every lookup or write of stored custom values by value in the project services compares exactly (bytes), never through the column collation: `RenameValueService` (the `CustomValue ... update_all(value: nv)` rewrite and its usage counts), `UsageCalculator.usage_here` / `usage_other` and `page_usage`, and any other `where(value: ...)` on `custom_values` in `app/` or `lib/`. One shared helper (for example next to `UsageCalculator.grouped_value_counts`, which already groups by the bytes of the value) used by every site; PostgreSQL and SQLite stay as they are (already exact). Renaming 'A' rewrites only stored 'A', never 'a', 'Á' or 'A ' (trailing space: decide and pin how MySQL PAD SPACE collations are handled); usage counts and the confirmation panel count exactly the rows that the rename rewrites. Mark SD-17 fixed in `large_lists_defects.md` and remove the README known-issue note.
- CSV import on an empty list (owner): on the admin form of a List (depending) field whose child list is still empty, the import panel is shown when the parent has values (the export panel stays hidden), with 'Add missing child values' preselected, so a new field can be filled from a file; project pages and Key/Value children are unchanged (no add-missing there).
- K6 (owner): a Security line in the 0.1.0 CHANGELOG for SD-16 (the project confirmation panel named dependent fields the user cannot see), in section 3 and the CHANGELOG, without internal ids; Fixed and Changed lines for SD-17 and the empty-list import.
- Extended API (owner): install `jcatrysse/redmine_extended_api` (public, clone it; never push to it) next to this plugin in copies of the 5.1 and 7.0 checkouts (never alter the shared PostgreSQL checkouts), run its own spec suite and this plugin's suite with both installed, and exercise its custom field endpoints for both depending formats end to end over HTTP (create, update, read; value_dependencies, default_value_dependencies, parent_custom_field_id, hide_when_disabled, enumerations; a cycle and a MySQL oversize give 422 through its API; the safe attributes of UD-15 still work). Record the result for UD-14 (the removed MappingBuilder, ParentMenuBuilder and after_custom_field_save dispatch must not be referenced by it) and any incompatibility as a defect with a proposed fix; fix it here only when the cause is in this plugin.

**Related decisions and defects**: SD-16, SD-17, UD-14, UD-15

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Owner rules: no version bump (`init.rb` unchanged, `version '0.0.16'`), no tag, no commit, push, PR or workflow dispatch; redmine_extended_api (`d103a2d`) stays read-only and clean and was installed only into copies. WP-33 has no Compatibility, Rollback or 'Open input' subsection: rollback is reverting the WP-33 changes (no migration, setting, route or locale key); no earlier WP left an open input for WP-33.
- SD-17 helper: `UsageCalculator.value_rows(field, values)` (one value or an Array; ids become Strings) returns `CustomValue.where(custom_field_id:, value:)` and, when `Redmine::Database.mysql?` (mysql2 and trilogy), adds `CAST(CONVERT(custom_values.value USING utf8mb4) AS BINARY) = ?` (`IN (?)` for a list), bound through Rails' quoting. The plain predicate stays as the first conjunct (byte equality implies equality under every collation, and an index an administrator added on `value` still serves). `binary_value_sql` builds the expression from `CustomValue.table_name`, and `grouped_value_counts` groups by it. Sites: `usage_total`, `usage_here` (so `usage_other`), the `page_usage` base and `RenameValueService#rename_list!` (`value_rows(field, old).update_all(value: nv)`); `RemoveValueService#gather_impact` and `guard_removal!` (`block_removal_when_used` included) and `UpdateEnumerationsService#deactivated_usage` follow through them. No other lookup of stored values by value is left in `app/` or `lib/` (static guard example). PostgreSQL and SQLite run the same SQL as before (to_sql equality example); SQL Server, for which `mysql?` is false, keeps the plain predicate and its collation (not tested by this plugin).
- Comparison rule (decided conservatively, recorded in defects SD-17): exact bytes, trailing spaces significant (NO PAD, as PostgreSQL), so a stored 'A ' is neither counted nor rewritten for 'A'; a trailing NBSP, NUL, a zero-width space, a full-width 'A', 'ss' for the sharp s and two different emoji are kept apart too. Not `COLLATE utf8mb4_bin` (PAD SPACE on MariaDB and MySQL 5.7, error 1253 on another character set) and not the deprecated `BINARY` operator. `CONVERT(... USING utf8mb4)` gives UTF-8 bytes whatever the column character set (utf8mb3 and latin1 values probed); bound values arrive in the UTF-8 connection encoding of database.yml. The usage counts stop at `USAGE_CAP + 1` while the update is unbounded, so the panel equals the rewritten rows below 50,000. A rename to the same value reports the matched rows (mysql2 `CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS`). No Upgrade-notes line: nothing is to be done on upgrade, and values merged by earlier renames stay merged.
- Behaviour changes, MySQL and MariaDB only: a rename rewrites and counts only the exact rows; a project-only field whose near miss is used in another project no longer asks for confirmation; `block_removal_when_used` no longer refuses a value because a near miss is stored; an enumeration id counts by its exact string. The confirmation flow, its markup and the SD-16 naming rule are unchanged.
- Empty-list import (owner): `Editor#render` shows the import panel when the child list is empty, the parent has values and `canAddValues()` holds (admin, list kind, `data-dcf-editor-allow-add-values="1"`, no child-values attribute, values source found); the export, toolbar, status and unlinked list stay hidden and the text `no_child_values` stays (no new locale key: the I18N map, the registry, locale parity and the 14 editor fixtures are unchanged). Preselection rule: in that state 'Add missing child values' is ticked unless the user changed the box on the page (`addMissingTouched`, set by its change event), so a render, the values debounce, Apply, Undo or a parent change never overrides the user's choice; Clear leaves it; a list that becomes non-empty keeps the box as it is; an untouched box is ticked again when the list is emptied; ticking from code clears a stale preview. No server, route, parameter, permission or data attribute change: one form post saves the new possible values and the links, pruned (D6) against the posted values (system example).
- CHANGELOG, word for word in the section 3 M3 list (appended) and 'Unreleased (0.1.0)' (flat bullets, last in their groups; the Security group sits between Removed and API): the Changed line for the empty-list import (WP scope 'Fixed and Changed lines'), the Fixed line for SD-17 and the Security line for SD-16 (owner K6; the WP-28 fix is not changed); 73 lines, by group 15, 32, 14, 2, 3, 1, 1, 5; no internal id. PC-73 [Security, SD-16], PC-74 [Fixed, SD-17], PC-75 [Changed] (quoted with `"`), the id sentence and two section 5 rows (project rename on MySQL, admin empty list). This block was written during and after the runs; no spec reads it. The `test.log` files of the four PostgreSQL checkouts, the two MariaDB checkouts and the three Extended API copies were truncated after the last run, together with the copies' development logs (they hold the probe keys); the probe servers and MariaDB were stopped.
- Review round 1 (fixer, full review): contracts-r1-1, security-r1-1 and semantics-r1-3, README Safety names PostgreSQL, SQLite, MySQL and MariaDB instead of 'every database' and says that SQL Server (not tested) compares with its column collation, as before; the `page_usage` comment says the same (no code change); the CHANGELOG line was already limited to MySQL/MariaDB. contracts-r1-2 and semantics-r1-2, the Deviations bullet above names the unmet Tests item (1 failure with the Extended API installed, SD-21, spec kept, owner to accept it or have the Extended API fixed) and that the project configuration routes under `/extended_api` were not probed; SD-21 Status and the UD-14 status say the same. tests-r1-1 and contracts-r1-5, the static guard is an allowlist (Specs above): it reads `{app,lib}/**/*.{rb,rake,erb}`, drops comment lines and ERB comments, selects the lines with `value:`, `[:value]`, `(:value`, `custom_values`, `CustomValue` or a quoted `value =` / `value IN`, and compares them with 17 listed lines (the parameter reads, the permit list and the audit and length message lines included), printing the lines not listed and the listed lines no longer found. tests-r1-4, the flash is asserted equal to `I18n.t(:notice_value_renamed)`. contracts-r1-8, 'empty lists elsewhere are unchanged' gains an admin List child without `data-dcf-editor-values-source` (import and export hidden, no add-missing box, `no_child_values`). contracts-r1-3, the quality protocol version targets read '0.1.0 (M2) = WP-15..WP-20 and WP-36, 0.1.0 (M3) = WP-21..WP-35'. contracts-r1-7, the spec set index says WP-01 to WP-36 (header and row 11) and names WP-33 to WP-35 in the M3 row and WP-36 in the M2 row. 'Open input from WP-33' (1) in WP-34 describes the allowlist. No production change besides the comment, no locale or characterization change.
- Review round 1, red: (1) the old guard misses all six reviewer shapes (a rename wrapped over two lines, `joins(:custom_values).where(custom_values: { ... value: v })`, `field.custom_values.where(..., value: v)`, an interpolated id, `arel_table[:value].eq(v)`; plain Ruby against its pattern). The new guard on PostgreSQL 7.0, with those lines in a temporary `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/zz_wp33_probe.rb`, one line in `lib/tasks/zz_wp33_probe.rake` and one in `app/views/zz_wp33_probe.html.erb` (whose ERB comment naming `CustomValue` is left out), gives `16 examples, 1 failure` and names all eight lines under 'not listed'; the probe files were removed (`git status` lists no untracked file). (2) The values-source case passes on the unchanged code (it pins unchanged behaviour); with `&& !!cfg.valuesSource` dropped from `canAddValues` (mirror copy) `npm test` gives `# pass 543 # fail 1`, 'empty lists elsewhere are unchanged ...' at the new assertion. (3) On MariaDB 7.0 with the rename notice changed to `notice_values_saved` the request example fails (`expected: "Value renamed."`, `got: "Values saved."`); the old assertion would pass under that change ('Values saved.' holds no value).
- Review round 1, evidence on the final tree (every summary `plugin_worktree_sha=98ff338f36670936d62638e62d3aea32f4732366 dirty=17`; the diff without this file and the status had the same sha256, `c896a896...` and `706764b7...`, before and after the matrix): subset 7.0 with `spec/characterization` and `spec/quality` `649 examples, 0 failures`; `.codex/test_matrix.sh --suite rspec --suite js --suite lint` `version=5.1 exit=0 suite=rspec exit=0 2691 examples, 0 failures  suite=js exit=0 # pass 544 # fail 0` and the same on 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0 (seeds 48281, 65186, 12265, 48749; `<work dir>/logs/<v>-rspec-20261103-<stamp>.log` with stamps 000449, 001127, 001925, 002711), `lint exit=0 SYNTAX PASS COMPAT PASS RATCHET PASS files=156 base_offenses=117 head_offenses=83 fixed=26`; system 5.1 and 7.0 `23 examples, 0 failures` (`5.1-system-20261103-003550.log`, `7.0-system-20261103-003634.log`); seeds 1, 4242 and 31337 for the three changed service and request spec files on 5.1 and 7.0, each `431 examples, 0 failures`; MariaDB 10.11: the three files on 7.0 `436 examples, 0 failures`, full suite 7.0 `2707 examples, 0 failures, 6 pending` (seed 14458, `<MariaDB work dir>/logs/7.0-rspec-20261103-000449.log`) and 5.1 `2707 examples, 0 failures, 6 pending` (seed 5964, `5.1-rspec-20261103-001153.log`), every pending reason starts with 'UD-38:', the server stopped afterwards; the G9 script (1) to (7) green (73 lines, by group 15, 32, 14, 2, 3, 1, 1, 5, no internal id, no UN row without a line, no dash added); `git diff HEAD -- spec/characterization init.rb .github` is empty, `git tag -l` prints nothing. **Definition of done**: the per-WP report of [`large_lists_quality_protocol.md`](large_lists_quality_protocol.md) (sections 1 to 9) with observed evidence for every applicable gate.

### WP-34: Core behaviour for required children in bulk edit (UD-10 changed) and server-side confirmation for shared and global fields (UD-23 changed)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | M | 2, 9 | WP-33 |

**Scope**

- UD-10 (owner, core behaviour): bulk edit of issues and time entries and the context-menu wizard no longer add the marked '(none)' option (`data-dcf-required-none`) for required managed children; the runtime no longer enables or selects it; a bulk change to a parent value that allows no values leaves required children as core does (the save of each issue whose required child is blank is refused by core's required check). Remove the option from the server markup, the runtime, the fixtures and the README; keep the WP-19 radio blank sentinel (`data-dcf-blank`), which is a separate feature. Check and document whether the server still accepts a blank required child while its parent value allows no values (pre-WP-19 behaviour) and keep that server rule unchanged unless it was added by WP-19 only for the option. Section 3, the CHANGELOG, PC and UN rows follow (the WP-19 Added/Changed line about the marked '(none)' option is removed or rewritten).
- UD-23 (owner, server-side confirmation): on a shared or global field (the scope banner case), a dependency save from the project dependency page (editor save and replace import alike) and a sort A-Z / Z-A need the server-side confirmation, as rename and remove do: the service raises `ConfirmationRequired` unless `confirm=1` is posted, before any write, and audits as rename and remove do. Dependency page: a required confirmation checkbox inside the form next to Save, shown only for shared and global fields (no re-post of the large payload through a panel); a missing confirmation re-renders the page with the posted mapping dirty (as the 422 path) and the confirmation text. Sort: the existing confirmation panel flow of the values page with the pending direction, state_hash and q; the browser confirm stays only for project-only fields, so no field asks twice. Never name a hidden field (SD-16 rule).

**Files**

- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/depending_format_methods.rb`, `assets/javascripts/depending_custom_fields.js`, `test/js/fixtures/**` (required-none removal)
- `app/services/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_mapping_service.rb`, `sort_values_service.rb`, `app/controllers/project_custom_field_configuration_controller.rb`, `app/views/project_custom_field_configuration/edit_dependencies.html.erb`, `_values_toolbar.html.erb`, `_confirm_panel.html.erb`, `assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js` (if the editor must carry `confirm`)
- `config/locales/*.yml`, specs (requests, services, system, jsdom), `CHANGELOG.md`, `README.md`, docs/specs/*.md

**Tests**

- Bulk edit and wizard of a required child: no '(none)' option in any state; parent set to a value without links: core refusal per issue (characterized); `data-dcf-blank` radio sentinel unchanged
- Shared and global field: dependency save and replace import without `confirm` give the confirmation, write nothing and keep the posted mapping dirty; with `confirm=1` they save; project-only fields save without it; sort without `confirm` shows the panel, with it sorts; no double prompt; hidden child never named
- System specs on 5.1 and 7.0 for bulk edit, wizard, shared-field dependency save and sort

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0; npm test; system specs on 5.1 and 7.0

**Open input from WP-33**: (1) The confirmation flow (`ConfirmationRequired`, `confirm=1`, `dcf_confirm_target`, `_confirm_panel.html.erb` with `UsageCalculator.nameable_child?`) is unchanged; its usage counts are byte-exact on MySQL and MariaDB since WP-33, and any new usage count of a sort or dependency-save confirmation goes through `UsageCalculator.value_rows` (the static guard in `spec/services/dcf_usage_calculator_spec.rb` lists every code line of `app/` and `lib/` that names `CustomValue`, `custom_values` or a value key and fails on any other; a new line is added to its list only when it does not look up stored values by value or goes through `value_rows`). (2) README Safety still says dependency saves, replace imports and the sort have no confirmation step; rewrite it. A new panel that names fields follows the SD-16 rule, whose Security line is now in section 3 and the CHANGELOG (PC-73, UN-66). (3) The 0.1.0 section has 73 lines (15, 32, 14, 2, 3, 1, 1, 5 by group); removing or rewriting the WP-19 '(none)' Added line (PC-39, UN-13) shifts the CHANGELOG line numbers, so rerun the G9 checks rather than reuse the WP-32 or WP-33 tables.

**Related decisions and defects**: UD-10, UD-23, SD-16

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Owner rules: no version bump (`init.rb` keeps `version '0.0.16'`), no tag added (`git tag -l` lists only the 13 upstream tags 0.0.1 to 0.0.14, without 0.0.6, unchanged), nothing to main, no workflow dispatch; `git diff ad5cb64 -- init.rb .github config/locales` is empty. Rollback is reverting the WP-34 changes (no migration, setting, route or locale key).
- UD-10, server markup: `DependingFormatMethods#bulk_edit_tag` is the pre-WP-19 body again (`git diff 3a323d8 -- lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/depending_format_methods.rb` shows only the WP-19 `edit_tag` sentinel hunk): one `ClientData.attributes_for(..., context: :bulk, ...)` and core's `super` with `ClientData.merge_options` for every field, so a required managed child has no `__none__` (core 5.1 `field_format.rb:603`, 7.0 `:615`) on issue bulk edit, bulk copy, time-entry bulk edit, the wizard template (`ContextMenuWizardHelper#render_custom_field` unchanged) and the failed bulk re-render (a posted `__none__` selects nothing). No element carries `data-dcf-required-none` (`grep -rn data-dcf-required-none lib app assets` prints nothing). The WP-19 radio sentinel (`edit_tag`, `ClientData.sentinel?`, `client_data.rb`, `dcf_sentinel_params_spec.rb`, `radio.html`, `radio_required.html`) is byte-identical.
- UD-10, runtime: `depending_custom_fields.js` drops `isRequiredNone`, the decide input `noneRequired`, the filter flag `requiredNoneEnabled` and the third parameter of `optionEnabled` (now `optionEnabled(filter, value)`, as frontend design 4 documents); every path behaves as `noneRequired=false` did. `grep -c 'required-none\|requiredNone\|noneRequired'` gives 0; ES2017 kept (`npm run check` 'ok ES2017' for the six assets), UMD shape and `window.DependingCustomFields` unchanged. A required child under parent '(none)' or a value without links: single '' selected (state `none` or `empty`, hint `empty` 'No values are available for the selected <parent>.'), multi nothing selected and nothing posted (FM-02), no auto-clear, no `bulkCleared`, no `dcf-auto-applied` (quality C7, the rejected outcome). The clear stops at a required field: its dependents read '' as '(No change)' (`parentState` 'unfiltered') and go back to their choice under '(No change)' (`decideUnfiltered`, the memory under `NOCHANGE_KEY`; review round 1, semantics-r1-3), not cleared; optional children below an optional parent are still cleared (UD-09).
- Fixtures: `DCF_WRITE_JS_FIXTURES=1` on 7.0 regenerated only `test/js/fixtures/markup/bulk_required.html` (one line removed; `git show 3a323d8:test/js/fixtures/markup/bulk_required.html | diff -` is empty); 36 markup fixtures, no version variant, the 14 editor fixtures and `wizard_template.html`, `radio.html`, `radio_required.html` unchanged. The fixture contract refuses `data-dcf-required-none` anywhere again (`not_to match(/data-dcf-(state|required-none)/)`); the `data-dcf-blank` checks are unchanged.
- Server rule kept: `patches/custom_field_patch.rb`, `dependency_rules.rb` and `spec/characterization/depending_formats_spec.rb` are unchanged (the 0.0.11 bypass, commit 0fcb2b6, older than WP-19). Observed per issue (bulk update, time-entry bulk update, wizard save; list and enumeration, single and multi; parent set to '__none__' or to 'C' without links, the required child left on '(No change)'): the record whose required child keeps a value is refused '<Child> is invalid: #id' (HTTP 200 core re-render; the wizard 422 after saving issue by issue) and keeps parent and child; the record whose required child is blank is saved with the new parent value; a parent value with links keeps core's 'cannot be blank'. Scripted '__none__' posts (and pages opened before the upgrade) behave as before (core and the wizard map them to ''). Recorded in UD-10 as 'Observed (WP-34)', the owner's sentence kept as written (open question 1 and 5: annotate, recommended).
- UD-23, one scope rule: `FieldRelevance.scope(field, project)` (:global for a ProjectCustomField or `is_for_all`, :shared when `field.projects` holds another project of any status, else :project) is the only definition (the same expression as before); `dcf_field_scope` (banner, badge, settings tab, toolbar, dependency page checkbox) and `BaseService#shared_or_global?` (rename, remove, dependency save, sort) call it. Decided per request, so a field that became shared after the GET asks.
- Confirm rule: `BaseService#confirmed?` is true only for the String '1' or 'true', case and surrounding white space ignored, compared as bytes (`CONFIRM_WORDS.include?(raw.b.strip.downcase)`; review round 1, security-r1-1, fail-closed; was String plus the `ActiveModel::Type::Boolean` cast, which also confirmed 'no', 'none', 'null', 'yes', 'on' and a blank). nil, '', ' ', '0', 'false', 'off', 'no', 'n', 'none', 'null', 'nil', 'yes', 'on', an invalid UTF-8 String, an Array, a Hash, `ActionController::Parameters`, an Integer and true do not confirm (never an ArgumentError). `value_params` still permits `:confirm` as a scalar; `dependency_params` passes `params[:confirm]` raw on the JSON and the nested path.
- Dependency save: `DependencyMappingService#perform!` calls `confirm_posted_mapping!` (`confirm_shared_change!`; on the legacy path a rescued `ConfirmationRequired` first stores a `DependencyPayload::Result` of the sanitized posted mapping as `parsed_payload`, no parse, review round 1) after the parent checks, `read_input!`, the sanitizers, `shape_defaults!` and `validate_mapping!`, before `DependencyDelta.new`, `assign_mapping` and `save_field!` (not in `validate_mapping!`). Order: preamble (403, 404, format, archived project, 409), parse 422, validation 422, confirmation, storage errors only on a confirmed save. Editor saves, replace and merge imports, no-op saves and the nested params alike; project-only fields ignore confirm. `BaseService#call` re-raises `ConfirmationRequired` without a row, so nothing is written and nothing audited (as rename and remove).
- Controller and page: `update_dependencies` rescues `ConfirmationRequired` (a StandardError, a 500 before) with `flash.now[:warning] = translate_error(:text_confirm_cross_project_change)`, `prepare_dependencies(posted: posted_payload(service))` and numeric 422: the posted mapping in `data-dcf-editor-mapping` with `data-dcf-editor-dirty="1"`, the hidden `dependencies_json` blank, the stored `state_hash` (R9); on the nested path the sanitized posted mapping, dirty, too (review round 1, semantics-r1-1; its validation and storage 422s keep the stored mapping, as before). `edit_dependencies.html.erb` renders, only for :shared and :global and only with the form, `label.dcf-confirm-box > check_box_tag :confirm, '1', false, required: true, autocomplete: 'off'` with `text_dcf_confirm_understand` in `p.buttons` before Save, outside the editor root and the shared partial: never pre-checked (literal false), not on the admin form, not on the parent notice pages. The editor script is unchanged (it posts the form natively; the browser blocks an unticked Save before the submit event).
- Sort: `SortValuesService` calls `confirm_shared_change!` after the direction check and before any write, also for an already sorted field; an invalid direction stays the audited 422. `perform_value_operation` renders the panel with numeric 422; `dcf_confirm_target(:sort_values)` gives `[custom_field_configuration_sort_values_path, :patch]`; `_confirm_panel.html.erb` has a sort branch (the rename and remove output is byte-identical: the branch lines are ERB statements trimmed by Erubi, the old lines not re-indented) with `text_confirm_cross_project_change`, `text_dcf_sort_confirm` (count of every value), hidden `direction` and the posted `state_hash` (`pending_params.slice`), `q` only when it filters (`list_state.slice(:q)`, never `page`), the unchecked required `confirm` box and `button_apply`; no usage lines, no `UsageCalculator` or `nameable_child?` call, names no field. `_values_toolbar.html.erb` gets the scope from `show.html.erb` and keeps `data-confirm` only for :project. `dcf_values_page.js` is unchanged (a sort button without `data-confirm` asks only the unsaved question while dirty, nothing when clean; jsdom pins).
- Unchanged without confirmation (owner scope: sort only): reorder_values, update_enumerations, add_value, set_default_value; `git diff ad5cb64 --stat` lists none of their services, nor `depending_custom_fields_api_controller.rb`, `context_menu_wizard_controller.rb`, `context_menu_wizard.js`, `sanitizer.rb`, `dependency_editor_config.rb`, `app/views/depending_custom_fields`, `dcf_dependency_editor*.js`. ### WP-35: '#<id>' cells in the enumeration CSV import (UD-19 changed)

| Release | Size | Points | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1.0 (M3) | S | 8 | WP-34 |

**Scope**

UD-19 (owner): for Key/Value list (depending) fields and Key/Value list parents, an import cell of the form '#<digits>' names a value of that field by id (active or inactive). Matching order per cell: an exact '#<id>' of a value of that field first; when no value of the field has that id, the usual name matching (so a value literally named '#12' still matches by name). Ids of other fields, unknown ids that also match no name, and malformed forms ('# 12', '#12a', '#-1', '#0') follow the name rules and are reported as unknown. The export writes '#<id>' instead of the name only for values whose name is ambiguous within the field (UD-19 duplicates), so export then import round-trips; formula protection treats '#' as no trigger. List fields are unchanged ('#12' is a plain value there). Preview, errors and the ambiguity message mention the '#<id>' way out. Pure client side: the payload still carries keys.

**Files**

- `assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor_model.js`, `assets/javascripts/dcf_dependency_editor.js`
- `lib/redmine_depending_custom_fields/dependency_editor_config.rb` (only if a text is added), `config/locales/*.yml`
- `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor_model.test.js`, `test/js/dcf_dependency_editor.test.js`, `test/js/fixtures/payload/*` (new import golden), `spec/requests/dependency_payload_contract_spec.rb`
- `CHANGELOG.md`, `README.md`, docs/specs/*.md

**Tests**

- '#<id>' on parent and child cells, inactive id, foreign id, unknown id, a value named '#12', malformed forms, ambiguity resolved by id, export of duplicates writes '#<id>' and re-imports to the same pairs, round-trip golden through the server contract spec

**Acceptance**

- Standard gate green on 5.1, 6.0, 6.1 and 7.0; npm test

**Open input from WP-33**: (1) The import on an empty child list is limited to List (depending) children on the admin form (`Editor#canAddValues`); a '#12' cell stays a plain value for List fields, also when 'Add missing child values' is preselected on an empty list, and the jsdom tests 'empty admin List child ...' must stay green. (2) The preselection rule: in the empty-list state the box is ticked unless the user changed it on the page (`addMissingTouched`); a change to the import panel keeps it. (3) Key/Value list (depending) entries cannot be created through redmine_extended_api (SD-18); README examples that need entries use this plugin's API or the administration form.

**Open input from WP-34**: (1) On the project dependency page a field shared with other projects or global needs the confirmation checkbox next to Save (UD-23 changed by the owner, WP-34): an import, '#<id>' cells included, is applied in the editor and then saved with the box ticked; the box lives outside the editor root and the shared partial, so the editor script and the 14 editor fixtures are unchanged. (2) `spec/requests/dependency_payload_contract_spec.rb` posts `confirm: '1'` on its global enumeration fields since WP-34 (Changed by WP-34 notes); a new round-trip golden saved through the project page needs it as well, or a project-only field. (3) A save waiting for the confirmation re-renders the posted mapping dirty without the 'source' and 'import' metadata (`DependencyEditorConfig.for_project`, as every 422), so a script that bypasses the box and re-posts from that page audits as source 'editor'.

**Related decisions and defects**: UD-18, UD-19

**Open input from WP-35**: (1) The unknown and ambiguity messages for Key/Value lists are the new keys `error_dcf_import_unknown_parent_id`, `error_dcf_import_unknown_child_id`, `error_dcf_import_ambiguous_parent_id` and `error_dcf_import_ambiguous_child_id` (JS `err_unknown_parent_id`, `err_unknown_child_id`, `err_ambiguous_parent_id`, `err_ambiguous_child_id`), chosen by `Editor#lineText` for `kind` `enumeration` (`ID_HINT_CODES`); the model error codes are unchanged and List fields keep the old keys. (2) `ValueList.match(cell, kind)` takes an optional kind; `candidates(list, cell, ids)` and `buildCsv` option `kind` are the only seams, so a later WP that reads a value list needs no change. (3) Quality protocol section 9 has UN-71 and the compatibility register PC-79.

**Delivered (decisions taken during implementation)**

- Scope: '#<id>' resolves only for `opts.kind === 'enumeration'` (parent and child, decided by the one editor kind, as `PARENT_FORMATS` keeps both lists in one family). List fields and `addMissing` never see id forms, so '#12' stays a plain value there; the 'empty admin List child' jsdom tests are unchanged and green.
- Pinned forms: an id cell is exactly `^#[1-9][0-9]*$` (ASCII digits, at most 19 digits and not above 9223372036854775807), tested on the cell after trim and optional unprotect, compared as text with `item.key` (no Number). '#007', '#0', '#', '# 12', '#12a', '#-1', '#+1', '#12.0', fullwidth and Arabic-Indic digits, inner or trailing spaces and numbers beyond 63 bits are not id forms: they go through the name steps and are reported `unknown_parent`/`unknown_child` with the cell unchanged when no name matches (owner confirmation of leading zeros not required by the documents, stated here). A hit is exactly one value of the same field (`byKey`, active or inactive); a foreign, hidden-field or unused id is reported exactly alike, only the field's own list is read.
- Order in one place (`candidates`): id lookup, then exact, NFC, NFC lowercase name; the id path builds no label index (25,000 values and rows, `labelIndex` stays null). Inactive id hits give the `inactive` warning with the cell. Name and id for one pair count as duplicates.
- Export: `buildCsv` option `kind`; for `enumeration` a value is written as '#<id>' when another value of the field shares its NFC lowercase name (so case twins and NFC/NFD twins too, decision: more conservative than the exact step) or when its label is the id form of a different value; otherwise the label. The count index is built once per list. The editor passes `kind` in `exportLinks` and `exportMine`. List kind output is byte-identical. '#' is no formula trigger: protect never prefixes it, and "'#12" is not unprotected to an id (it is a name).
- Texts: `text_dcf_import_help` mentions '#<id>' (all four locales, registry row); two new keys (en, de, fr, nl, `DependencyEditorConfig::I18N` 100 to 102, registry rows and totals) are used for Key/Value lists only, the old ambiguity keys stay for List fields (decision on open question 1). Unknown messages unchanged (the help text carries the form); changed in review round 1 (below): two more keys name the form in the unknown messages too. The 14 editor fixtures regenerated (only the `data-dcf-editor-i18n` attribute).
- Contract golden: `test/js/fixtures/payload/import_merge_ids.json` (merge mode, enumeration pair with a duplicate Alpha, five '#<id>' and name rows); the contract spec derives the mode with `delete_suffix('_ids')` and the stale-guard example resets the fixture counter per golden. The five existing goldens are byte-identical.
- Docs: CHANGELOG line and section 3 line (word for word, after the CSV import line), README bullet, PC-79, UN-71, editor design 6.3 and 6.7, decisions UD-19 and D7, i18n registry. No server, view, payload, State, parser, serialize or applyImport change.
- Changed existing examples: `planImport: enumerations by name only ... no #id syntax` (retitled, the '#9200004' assertion now links, flip annotated 'Changed by WP-35'); 'import errors ... ambiguous' in `dcf_dependency_editor.test.js` expects `err_ambiguous_parent_id` (annotated); `editor_fixtures.test.js` and `dependency_editor_config_spec.rb` key lists and the I18N size 102. `git diff e3248a8 --stat -- spec/characterization` is empty.
